## Review

**8 out of 10, Strong.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck states one four-part case on slide 6 and carries it consistently through the section dividers, the top navigation tabs (slides 25 to 54) and the close on slide 65, so the audience can follow the argument across 69 pages. Action titles say the point, the navy and teal palette is applied consistently, and the data slides are readable: slide 9 pairs a clean bar chart with four large KPI callouts, and slide 29 gives margin context under each bar. Weaknesses are moderate: slide 42 has an awkward white band across the middle, uneven empty space and an unequal driver column, several diagram slides run small text, and some content repeats (the pillar slides at 6, 22 and 65, the target table at 19 and 60, the cost savings on 18, 53 and 54).

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent title-plus-callout-panel layout, the numbered four-pillar framing on slide 6, the chart treatments on slides 9 and 29, and the section tabs and dividers that orient the audience.

**Most useful repair:** Tidy slide 42 by removing the stray white band and balancing the driver columns, and enlarge or simplify the tiny logo walls on slide 26, the small text on slide 47 and the footnote type throughout.

**What would change the score:** I would cut or merge the repeated pillar, target and cost-savings slides, and make the navigation tabs more legible, since the inactive tabs are nearly invisible.

_Confidence high. 69 of 69 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a cover slide for the 2026 Investor Day, followed by a forward-looking statements disclaimer and a day agenda listing speakers from the CEO, merchandising, digital, operations and finance. The company is introduced as the spin-off of ADI from Resideo, a specialty distributor of low-voltage products, with standalone FY2025 revenue and adjusted EBITDA shown beside the building products company being spun off. The strategy section sets out four pillars: category leadership, an omnichannel platform, multiple growth levers and margin upside. Subsequent sections cover the products and suppliers offer, the omnichannel customer experience, operations and trusted partner service, and financial performance. The financial section presents historical results, 2030 targets, capital allocation and pro forma capitalization. The appendix reconciles non-GAAP EBITDA figures. Slides worth studying include the strategy overview on slide 6, the revenue and M&A growth chart on slide 9, and the digital growth algorithm on slide 42.

## Questions about this deck

**What are the 2030 revenue and margin targets for ADI Global Distribution Inc.?**

Slide 19 sets the 2030 targets as revenue of ~$6B and Adj. EBITDA margin of >8%, up from $4.8B and 6.2% in 2025A. The slide also lists a target of >22.7% gross margin and cumulative cash flow from operations of $1B+ for FY2026-2030.

**How many slides are in the ADI Global Distribution Inc. 2026 Investor Day deck?**

The deck has 69 slides (pages). Slide 1 is the cover for the ADI 2026 Investor Day on July 14, 2026, and slide 2 contains the forward-looking statements disclaimer.

**What market size does ADI Global Distribution Inc. cite for North America in its investor deck?**

Slide 11 shows a North America Total Addressable Industry (TAI) of about $65B in 2025, with an estimated industry growth CAGR of +MSD% (mid single digit) for 2025 to 2030. The slide attributes this to company management analysis.

**How is the strategy of ADI Global Distribution Inc. structured in its investor deck?**

Slide 6 summarizes the strategy in four pillars: Category-leading Positions, Preeminent Omnichannel Platform, Multiple Levers for Above Market Growth, and Attractive Margin Upside Potential & Cash Flow Generation. Slide 16 expands on the growth levers, including category leadership, omnichannel experience, exclusive brands, expansion categories, value-added services and execution excellence.

**Visuals**

Subject: several blue freestanding fixture displays with product signage: at left, partially visible white panels reading 'Hanwha Vision' and 'LUMA'; a white panel reading 'resideo'; a blue-and-white display with the AXIS COMMUNICATIONS logo and yellow-and-white security camera boxes; a central large blue display with a wall-mounted screen showing a hand and the text 'Uniformly and securely integrate Era 100 Pro into your projects', a wooden tray holding Sonos boxes and an 'Era 100 Pro' box below. Dominant colours: royal blue, white, grey floor, orange and wood-brown floor at left. A navy #1A4366 tint at about 32% opacity overlays the photograph. Below the photograph, a flat navy #1A4366 panel. Small white ADI logo (wordmark with stylised slanted letters) at upper-left of panel, no other logos.

**Layout**

Two-part slide: a full-width light grey-blue band across the top 56% holds the title at top left and a row of five centred circular portraits with name and role captions beneath them. The lower white area holds two columns of agenda rows (time, session, speaker) separated by thin teal rules, with the ADI logo at bottom right and the page number at bottom right corner; the title sits left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge and the content margins are roughly 4% left and 4% right.

**Visuals**

Five circular headshot photographs in a single row, each about 225 px in diameter on the 2000 px wide slide, centred over its caption. From left: Rob Aarnes, a smiling man with short light-brown hair in a navy blazer over a light blue open-collared shirt, indoors with blurred office windows behind; Marco Cardazzi, a smiling bald man with stubble in a dark jacket over a checked shirt, with a red exit sign and ceiling light blurred behind; Stu Tisdale, a smiling man with short dark hair in a blue-and-white checked blazer over a light blue shirt, with a blurred grey office background; Allie Copeland, a smiling woman with long dark wavy hair in a dark top, with a bright window behind; Kevin Prush, a smiling bearded man with short dark hair in a grey-blue blazer over a white shirt, against a blurred green-grey office. All portraits are head-and-shoulders, centred, cropped to circles, with soft natural daylight and no overlay. Thin teal horizontal rules (about 1 px, #2380A3) divide the agenda rows: three rules in the left column at about 66%, 76% and 85% of slide height and three in the right column at the same heights. A small square image (220 by 220 px) sits behind the logo area with no visible content, and the ADI logo is a bold navy sans-serif wordmark in #1A4366 with angled letterforms.

**Design**

Background: Two-zone solid: #E5E9EF light grey-blue over top 56% and #FFFFFF white below. Upper band light blue-grey #E5E9EF; lower area white #FFFFFF. Title is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #1A4366 at about 5.6% of slide height. Speaker names are Arial Bold in teal #2380A3 at about 2.9%; role captions are Arial Regular in navy #1A4366 at about 2.5%. Agenda times are Arial Regular in teal #2380A3 at about 2.5%; session titles are Arial Bold in navy #1A4366 at about 2.5%; speakers in agenda are Arial Bold in teal #2380A3. Page number and footer text are small Arial at about 1.9%. Rules are thin teal lines. Portraits are circular with no border or shadow. No gradients, no corner radii on shapes.

4

**Layout**

Two symmetrical columns of equal circular panels, left panel red (Resideo), right panel navy (ADI), with a central spin-distribution column between them. The title sits top-left at about x 3.9 percent, y 6.1 percent, left aligned, spanning about 58 percent of the width. Page number 4 sits at the bottom-right corner, the ADI logo sits at bottom-right above it, and a two-line grey note runs across the bottom-left margin at about y 95 percent.

**Visuals**

Two circular photographs and a central logo with connector arrows. Left circle (about x 3.3, y 27.2, 36.1 by 64.0 percent): a family of four, a woman and a man with two children, gathered on a sofa looking at a tablet in a bright home interior, faded with a white wash, cropped to a circle with a red outline. Right circle (about x 60.6, y 27.3, 36.1 by 63.9 percent): warehouse shelving with stacked boxes in aisles, faded with a white wash, cropped to a circle with a navy outline. Centre: REZI LISTED NYSE logo in black block letters with two horizontal rules inside a white rounded square with grey border and soft shadow. A red arrow runs left from the logo and turns down to the top of the left circle; a navy arrow runs right and turns down to the top of the right circle. Logos: resideo wordmark in black, ADI wordmark in navy, ADI small navy logo bottom-right. Six circular metric badges: three red (#D8282F) under the Resideo headline and three navy (#153655) under the ADI headline, each with a bold white value over italic white labels. No chart or table.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Arial sans-serif, navy #1A4366, large at about 5.6 percent of slide height. Headlines in bold italic Roboto, about 3.3 percent of height: red #D8282F for the Resideo line and dark slate #242E38 for the ADI line. Spin Distribution label in bold italic Roboto, underlined, black #000000. Body text in italic Roboto, black, about 2.4 percent. Badge values bold Roboto white about 2.5 percent; badge labels italic Roboto white about 1.6 to 1.7 percent. Footnote in regular Arial grey #7F7F7F at about 1.3 percent. Connector lines 0.3 percent thick, red #D22730 and navy #153755. Badges have soft drop shadows and rounded circular shapes. No gradients.

### Slide 5

**Text**

The ADI Strategy / Be the indispensable / Partner of choice / Rob Aarnes / President & Chief Executive Officer / Company Confidential / 5

**Layout**

Title slide with all text left-aligned at about 6.9% from the left edge in a column that runs from about 39% to 93% of the slide height. The title block sits in the upper-middle, the presenter name and role sit near the bottom left, and the ADI logo sits bottom right at about 82% to 94% of the width. A small confidentiality line sits bottom left and a page number sits in the bottom-right corner. No grid is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background illustration: a blue gradient with a faint, thin-outlined geometric pattern of repeated chevrons and triangles running diagonally from upper left to lower right. The pattern is a darker blue line work over the gradient. Lighter teal-blue at the top right fades to deeper navy at the bottom left. Composition is edge to edge with no cropping of a subject. Dominant colours are teal-blue #1F7A9C, mid blue #1F5F80 and navy #17405F. The ADI wordmark logo is white, in the bottom right: a bold sans-serif 'ADI' in which the A and the D have slanted diagonal cuts. A 220 by 220 pixel image box is placed at x 88.7, y 86.7, w 7.5, h 13.3 with no visible content. No chart, table or photograph.

**Design**

Background is a blue gradient with a subtle outlined triangle pattern, with no solid colour block. Title text is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold, sans-serif, weight bold, set at about 7.5% of slide height per line, with line spacing of about 8.9% between baselines. The kicker 'The ADI Strategy' is Arial Regular in teal #66D7D1 at about 4.1% of slide height. Presenter name is Arial Bold white at about 4.4%. Role line is Arial Regular white at about 3.3%. Confidentiality line is Arial Italic grey #7F7F7F at about 1.9%. Page number is Arial Regular white at about 1.9%. No rules, shapes, corner radii, shadows or card boxes. Margins are about 7% left.

### Slide 6

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A four-column summary of the strategy pillars with a short headline and one-line support under each, which makes the whole argument readable at a glance.
- Review: The deck states one four-part case on slide 6 and carries it consistently through the section dividers, the top navigation tabs (slides 25 to 54) and the close on slide 65, so the audience can follow the argument across 69 pages.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent title-plus-callout-panel layout, the numbered four-pillar framing on slide 6, the chart treatments on slides 9 and 29, and the section tabs and dividers that orient the audience.

**Text**

ADI is a differentiated leader in an attractive market
driving margin expansion and cash flow generation
1 Category-leading Positions
Category leadership positions in a large and growing addressable industry
2 Preeminent Omnichannel Platform
Premier one-stop shop experience built on knowing Pros and meeting their needs
3 Multiple Levers for Above Market Growth
Growing revenue above market with multiple organic levers to win and inorganic upside
4 Attractive Margin Upside Potential & Cash Flow
Recent platform investments and standalone focus support EBITDA and cash flow growth
Company Confidential
6

Four horizontal chevron-banded rows, identical in structure. Each row has a solid navy (#1A4366) chevron block with a white outlined circle containing a thin white numeral (1, 2, 3, 4) at the left of the block, followed by a white bold two-line heading; the block's right edge is a point (arrow tip) pointing right into a light grey (#E5E9EF) rectangle that holds a navy two-line body statement. No photographs, charts or icons apart from the ADI wordmark logo in the bottom-right corner, which is a navy (#1A4366) stylised bold sans-serif 'ADI' letterform mark. ### Slide 7

**Text**

Our scale and industry leadership are long-term advantages
ADI is an industry leader …
Leading North America specialty distributor in three categories:
#1 Security
#1 Residential AV
#1 Fire/Life Safety
… with global reach …
4,100+ Global Workforce
160 Americas Store Locations
38 International Store Locations
Physical Locations
Serviced Regions
… and platform scale.
~100K Customers
$4.8B ‘25A Revenue
Revenue By Geography
International 12%
Americas 88%
Revenue By Brand
Exclusive Brands 18%
Third-Party Brands 82%
7
Note: all values based on FY2025
Source: Company Management Analysis, SDM 100, CEPro 100
Company Confidential

**Layout**

Two-column split: a wide light-grey top band and white lower-left area hold the title, subtitle, three category pills and a world map, while a full-height dark navy right panel (about 26% of width) holds platform stats and two donut charts. Title sits top-left at about 4% left margin; the slide number sits bottom-right of the dark panel and footnotes sit bottom-left, both about 2% from the edges.

**Visuals**

Illustration: a world map occupying roughly the lower-left area, with countries coloured by status. Dark navy #1A4366 marks physical locations, which include the United States, Canada, Alaska, the UK, France, Spain, Germany, Scandinavia, Australia and New Zealand. Teal #66D7D1 marks serviced regions, which include Mexico, Central and South America, much of Africa, the Middle East, Central Asia, China, India, Southeast Asia and Indonesia. Light grey #EAEAEA marks unserviced regions, including Greenland, northern Russia and parts of Africa. Borders are thin white lines. Legend below the map: a dark navy swatch labelled Physical Locations and a teal swatch labelled Serviced Regions. Donut chart 1, titled Revenue By Geography: two segments, Americas 88% in teal #2380A3 (large arc) and International 12% in white (small arc starting at 12 o'clock); inner hole dark #2C4051 about half the outer diameter; no axes or legend, categories labelled directly. Donut chart 2, titled Revenue By Brand: Third-Party Brands 82% in teal #2380A3 and Exclusive Brands 18% in white, same geometry. Three white rectangular pills in the top band with values #1, #1, #1 for Security, Residential AV and Fire/Life Safety. Two white outlined boxes in the dark panel holding ~100K Customers and $4.8B ‘25A Revenue. Two thin white horizontal rules under the chart headings. An embedded 220 by 220 pixel raster image sits at the lower-right of the dark panel, with no visible content in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Split background: white #FFFFFF left area with pale #E5E9EF top band, and solid dark slate #2C4051 right panel. Left content area is white with a pale blue-grey #E5E9EF band across the top 40% of the slide; right panel is solid dark slate #2C4051. Titles are bold Arial (sans-serif) in dark navy #1A4366; body text is regular Arial in #1A4366; accents and big numbers are bold Arial in blue #2380A3, with teal #66D7D1 used on the map and stat accents. Title text is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle and section headers about 3.7%, body about 2.5%, footnotes about 1.7 to 1.9%. Pills are flat white rectangles with square corners and no outline. Stat boxes have thin white outlines and square corners. Lines are thin white rules. No shadows or gradients.

**Text**

Note: Data as of FY 2025

**Layout**

Two-part slide on a white field. A full-width title sits top-left in two left-aligned lines starting at about 6% from the top and 4% from the left margin. Below it, two section labels are centred over two groups of category tabs: Commercial Buildings on the left (about 22% to 58% across) and Residential Homes on the right (about 69% to 91%). Two illustrations fill the middle band, commercial left and residential right. A row of four colour-coded boxes runs along the bottom at about 77% to 97% height, with the 'Note' footnote bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two architectural cutaway illustrations with circular and rectangular callout highlights, plus four KPI boxes and two rows of coloured category tabs. (1) Commercial cutaway: a two-storey building section with a warehouse ground floor (roller shutter doors, pallet racking, forklift, stacked boxes) and an upper office floor (meeting room, server rack, reception). Roughly 20 callouts are placed on it: navy-ringed dome cameras, slate-ringed fire alarm and sensor units, teal-ringed speakers and a wall-mounted speaker, blue-ringed wireless access points and ceiling units, rectangular outlines around a server rack (blue), a display and conference bar (teal), a wall display (blue, showing a red abstract image) and a rack-mounted unit (teal). Lighting is flat and even; dominant colours are grey concrete, beige, white and warm wood tones. The image is boxed, unbordered, on a near-white field. (2) Residential cutaway: a gabled house section showing a bedroom loft with bunk beds, living room with sofa and TV, kitchen and dining area, staircase and an attached garage with a silver car. About 12 callouts: teal, slate and navy rings over ceiling sensors, a speaker, a keypad, a camera and a fire alarm. The image is unboxed, roughly 90% of its width within the frame, with a grey ground slab. (3) Category tabs: Commercial row of four blocks (Security navy, AV teal, Fire / Life Safety slate, Data Communications blue); Residential row of three blocks (Security navy, AV teal, Fire / Life Safety slate). (4) Four bottom boxes: Security (navy), Audio Visual (teal, wider), Fire / Life Safety (slate), Data Communications (blue), each with a revenue share; the AV and Data Communications boxes carry an expansion-category icon, a circle with an upward-right arrow, in white outline. A thin white vertical divider separates Residential (ResiAV) from Commercial (ProAV) inside the AV box.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the whole slide.

### Slide 9

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Pairs a revenue bar chart with callouts for organic and acquisition-driven growth, separating the two sources of growth visually.
- Review: Action titles say the point, the navy and teal palette is applied consistently, and the data slides are readable: slide 9 pairs a clean bar chart with four large KPI callouts, and slide 29 gives margin context under each bar.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent title-plus-callout-panel layout, the numbered four-pillar framing on slide 6, the chart treatments on slides 9 and 29, and the section tabs and dividers that orient the audience.

3 Segmented Adj. EBITDA does not include an allocation of historical corporate costs or estimated standalone costs associated with ADI operating as a separate / company. This measure is therefore not directly comparable to Standalone Adj. EBITDA presented elsewhere in this presentation
Source: Company Reported Financials, Management Analysis
9

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a wide left content area (about 0 to 74 percent of width) holds the title, chart and footnotes, and a full-height dark navy panel fills the right 26 percent holding a boxed period label, four stacked KPI figures and a final count. The two-line title sits top-left at about 4 percent left margin and 6 percent top, left-aligned, with the chart below it and the footnotes and source line stacked along the bottom left; the page number 9 sits bottom-right inside the dark panel.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart, eight bars for 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 (footnote 1) and 2025 (footnote 1), all in teal #2380A3 with no gridlines and no y-axis; bars rise left to right with heights about 2018 lowest and 2025 tallest. Printed values only: $2.7B label above the 2018 bar and $4.8B label above the 2025 bar; no values are printed for the other bars, and no axis units or ranges are shown. Chart title 'Reported ADI Revenue FY 2018 - 2025' in teal at upper left of the plot. No legend. Highlighted points: the 2018 and 2025 bars carry the printed value labels. Behind the bars sits a pale light-blue upward arrow-like freeform shape in #98D3E9 at about 39 percent opacity, rising from the lower left under the 2018 bar to a point near the top right above the 2025 bar, overlapping bars 2019 to 2025. Baseline: a thin dark navy #1A4366 horizontal axis line under the bars. Right panel: four white KPI figures with captions and one count figure, no icons or logos; a white thin-outlined rectangle frames the 'FY 2018 - 2025' label. A 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at the bottom right of the dark panel but is not discernible in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a full-height dark slate-navy #2C4051 panel on the right quarter. Titles and chart labels in dark navy #1A4366; accent teal #2380A3 for bars and the subtitle; white for panel text; grey #7F7F7F for footnotes and source. Typefaces: Arial Bold for titles, values and bar labels; Arial Regular for captions, year labels, footnotes and subtitle; both are sans-serif. Title about 5.6 percent of slide height, KPI figures about 7.5 percent, body and captions about 2.5 percent, footnotes about 1.7 percent. Lines: single thin baseline in navy. Shapes: square-cornered bars, a white rectangle outline with no fill around the period label, no corner radii, no shadows, no gradients.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Our capabilities are differentiated and difficult to replicate / reinforcing our competitive advantage / Local Availability / ~200 stores1 supported by / strong network of distribution centers / Deep Assortment / 1K+ brands and / 500K+ products / Digital Platforms / $1.4B+ revenue with ~75% / of customers digitally-engaged2 / Exclusive Brands / $800M+ revenue3 across / over a dozen valued brands / Trusted Expertise / ~1.7K elite sales team / members with industry expertise / Value-Added Services / 20+ valued / services offered / Leading Pros know they can rely on ADI / 1 ~200 stores represents store count as of FY 2025; this number is expected to be optimized with shared-market store combinations for a target count around 160 in current footprint / 2 ~75% of ADI Americas customers purchasing each week have a digital touchpoint – Q1 2026 data / 3 FY2025 Exclusive Brands revenue / Company Confidential / 10

**Layout**

Two-by-three grid of six equal rounded panels centred on the slide, with a circular ADI logo badge overlapping the centre where the four inner corners meet. The title sits top-left in two left-aligned lines at the top margin. Panels in the left column have left-aligned text and panels in the right column have right-aligned text. Footnotes sit left-aligned in three small lines above a full-width dark footer bar carrying the tagline centred; the page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six flat rounded rectangles in #2380A3 arranged as a 2 by 3 grid, each holding a heading and two lines of text, with no icons, photographs or charts. A central dark circle in #2C4051 with a thick white outline carries the white ADI logo, a stylised slanted wordmark in which the A, D and I letters are cut at angles. The panels' inner corners are hidden under the circle. A 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at the footer bottom right but is not visible in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif (Arial Bold) in dark blue #1A4366, about 5.6 percent of slide height for each line. Panel headings in bold white Arial, about 3.7 percent of slide height; panel body text in regular white Arial with bold numerals, about 2.9 percent. Panels are #2380A3 with rounded corners of roughly 1.5 percent of slide width, no shadow, no outline, separated by white gutters of about 0.8 percent. Central circle #2C4051 with white ring about 1 percent thick. Footer bar #2C4051 full width with white bold tagline in Arial about 4.1 percent. Footnotes in grey Arial about 1.7 percent of slide height.

11

**Layout**

Two-column split on a 16:9 slide. A white left area holds the title top-left (left edge about 3.9% of width), a subtitle beneath it, and a donut chart centred around x 36% with a teal callout at right and a grey callout at lower left. A full-height dark navy panel occupies the right 26.3% of the slide and stacks three centred statistics vertically. Footnotes and source line sit bottom-left at about 3.9% margin; the page number sits bottom-right inside the navy panel.

**Visuals**

One donut chart titled North America Total Addressable Industry (TAI), 2025, with a white centre disc reading ~$65B over TAI. Segments clockwise from top: Security & Fire about 15% (#2380A3 fill, white label), ResiAV about 10% (#1A4366 fill, white label, superscript 1), ProAV about 50% (#AFABAB fill, navy label, superscript 1), DataComm about 25% (#E7E6E6 fill, navy label). The outer ring is light grey #D0CECE and the top-right arc of it, spanning the Security & Fire and ResiAV segments, is teal #66D7D1 and is highlighted, with a teal callout box reading Category Leader attached to it. A grey callout box at lower left reading Emerging Player with an italic line Expansion Categories and a small circular arrow icon (navy outline, diagonal up-right arrow) marks the emerging categories, pointing to the grey ring. Right of the donut, in the navy panel: a large white bold ~$65B with North America TAI beneath, a white downward chevron (thin stroke, about 4% wide), and a large white bold +MSD% with Estimated Growth CAGR2 beneath. A placed image at bottom right (220 by 220 pixels) is not visibly distinguishable on the render. No axes or legend.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the slide, with a navy #2C4051 panel covering the right 26.3%. White background with a dark navy #2C4051 right panel. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) navy #1A4366, subtitle in regular Arial #1A4366. Title size about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 3.3%, body and labels about 2.9% to 3.7%, footnotes about 1.7% to 1.9%. Stat numerals are Arial Bold white at about 7.5%. Accent teal #66D7D1 for the highlighted arc and callout; teal callout has rounded corners on its right side. Grey callout #D0CECE with rounded corners. Donut segments flat-filled with no visible outlines. No shadows or gradients.

12

**Layout**

Single-slide three-band layout on a white field. The title sits top-left, left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge and 6.1% from the top, with empty space below it. Below the title, three full-width horizontal bands span x 3.2% to 96.9%: a navy top band (y 23.5% to 36.8%), a light grey-blue middle band (y 36.8% to 63.5%) holding three icon-and-statistic groups in three columns, and a slate bottom band (y 67.2% to 80.5%). Each band has a label column at about x 8% and a statement column starting at about x 38.6%. Sources run as a two-line footnote along the bottom left (y 94.9% to 98.6%), 'Company Confidential' sits bottom left at the same baseline band, the ADI logo sits bottom right, and the page number sits at the far bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three outline line icons in navy #1A4366 on the light grey band. (1) Left: a commercial building icon of three rectangular buildings of differing heights, drawn with a square-window grid on each facade and a ground line, at about x 6.9% y 44.2% w 6.1% h 9.1%. (2) Centre: a calendar icon, a rectangle with two binder tabs at the top and a 3-by-2 grid of cells in the body, at about x 37.9% y 43.7% w 5.6% h 10.0%. (3) Right: a house icon group, three small house outlines (two at left and top, one at lower right) with a curved road or arrow sweeping from bottom left to upper right behind them, at about x 70.6% y 45.2% w 6.7% h 9.5%. Bottom right: the ADI logo, the letters ADI in bold navy #1A4366 slanted italic-style sans-serif, at about x 91.7% y 91.2% w 5.0% h 3.6%. A second placed image 220 by 220 pixels sits at x 88.7% y 86.7% w 7.5% h 13.3%, overlapping the logo area with no distinct visible content.

**Design**

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a single-line-height title block at the top left (two lines, left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge) above a large table spanning about 3.2% to 96.9% of the width and from about 28.5% to 88.6% of the height. A navy-outlined rectangle highlights the Short term and Medium term columns under a 'Macro-driven outlook' heading; a legend of four arrow-circle icons sits top right above the table. Footnotes are left-aligned at the bottom left, the ADI logo sits bottom right and the slide number sits at the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

A table with five columns (End market, ADI Category, Short term, Medium term, Current focus) and six data rows in two groups. Commercial group rows: Security | short term +MSD icon | medium term +MSD icon | Continue to execute and lead; AV | +MSD | +MSD | Invest, accelerate growth; Fire | +MSD | +MSD | Continue to execute and lead; DataComm | +MSD | +MSD | Invest, accelerate growth. Residential group rows: Security | short term Flat icon | medium term +LSD icon | Position for growth as macro normalizes; AV | short term -LSD icon | medium term +LSD icon | Position for growth as macro normalizes. Icons are outlined circles (about 2.7% of slide width) containing a single arrow: +MSD is a teal-blue #1F7FA3 circle with an up-right diagonal arrow; +LSD is a slate grey-blue #7C8DA6 circle with a right-slightly-up arrow; Flat is a gold #D4B020 circle with a horizontal right arrow; -LSD is a red-orange #E8583A circle with a down-right arrow. Legend top right reads + MSD (teal icon), + LSD (grey icon), Flat (gold icon), - LSD (red icon) in a single row. No numeric values are printed.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in Arial Bold, navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height per line, sentence case. Table header row in Arial Bold white #FFFFFF text, about 2.9% of slide height, on a slate blue #7C93AD band. Table body cells use a pale blue-grey #E5E9EF fill for the Commercial group; body text in Arial regular navy #1A4366 about 2.9% of slide height; the Residential group sits on white. Row dividers are thin slate lines #7C93AD. The Macro-driven outlook highlight is a navy #1A4366 rectangle outline about 0.3% of slide width thick with square corners and no fill. Group labels Commercial and Residential are Arial Bold navy. Footnotes are Arial regular grey #7E7E7E about 1.7% of slide height. No shadows or gradients.

### Slide 14

**Text**

Industry trends point towards increased importance in ADI’s areas of strength and focus / Key industry trends / ADI’s focus to win / Channel Convergence¹ / Digital Emergence² / Business Model Evolution³ / Technology Acceleration⁴ / Specialized in four converging categories / Investing to unlock digital growth while enabling omnichannel flexibility / Exclusive Brands, platforms, and services / Built to scale and adopt more AI + technology / Company Confidential / Sources: Company Management Analysis of Customer Surveys and Third-party Reports / 1 Commercial Integrator, AV & Security Convergence (2025); 2 Modern Distribution Management (MDM), B2B Digital Buying Trends (2025); 3 Modern Distribution Management (MDM) – Redwood / Advisors, Value-Added Services in Distribution (2025); 4 Distribution Strategy Group, State of AI in Distribution (2026) / 14

**Layout**

Two-column grid of four matched rows below a two-line title, with the title flush left at about 3.9% from the left edge and the column headers sitting above the rows. The left column holds four solid coloured label boxes, a small navy chevron and two dashed vertical rules form a central divider, and the right column holds four outlined statement boxes of equal width; the sources block runs along the bottom left, with the ADI logo and page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Four solid rectangular label blocks in the left column, each with white bold centred text: navy #1A4366 'Channel Convergence¹', teal-blue #2380A3 'Digital Emergence²', slate grey-blue #566E89 'Business Model Evolution³', and turquoise #31BCB5 'Technology Acceleration⁴'. Four outlined rectangles in the right column (no fill, stroke about 2.3 pt, square corners), each holding one line of bold text in the same colour as its matching block, in order: navy 'Specialized in four converging categories'; teal-blue 'Investing to unlock digital growth while enabling omnichannel flexibility'; slate 'Exclusive Brands, platforms, and services'; turquoise 'Built to scale and adopt more AI + technology'. Between the columns sits a navy #1A4366 triple chevron (three right-pointing chevrons in a row) at about the vertical centre of the second and third rows, and two dashed navy vertical lines: the upper one runs from the top of row one to the middle of row two, the lower one from the middle of row three to the bottom of row four. Bottom right is the ADI logo in navy, made of two stacked images, a square frame and the 'ADI' wordmark sitting inside it. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background #FFFFFF with no gradient, texture or imagery. Title in Arial Bold (grotesque sans-serif, bold weight), navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height, set on two lines. Column headers and the right-hand statements are in Arial regular and bold respectively: headers regular navy #1A4366 about 3.3% of slide height; statements bold about 3.7%, each in its accent colour. Label text is Arial Bold white about 3.3%. Sources and notes are Arial regular grey #7E7E7E about 1.7% of slide height; the page number is Arial regular #2C4051 about 1.9%; the confidentiality line is Arial italic grey #7F7F7F about 1.9%. Shapes are flat with square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Palette: navy #1A4366, teal-blue #2380A3, slate #566E89, turquoise #31BCB5.

15

**Layout**

Four equal dark-blue columns sit side by side across the upper two-thirds of the slide, each with a white-outlined heading box near its top and a bold white caption beneath. A teal chevron panel fills the lower band beneath the columns and carries the Execution heading and caption, with its apex rising into the gap between the middle columns. The title is left-aligned at top with a subtitle directly below it, both starting at about 3.9% from the left edge, and the page number sits at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five icons, no chart, table or photograph. Experience: a white outline of a mobile phone with a shopping cart on its screen. Innovation: a white box with an up-arrow and circular arrows around it, suggesting a parcel in motion. Expansion: a white circle outline containing a diagonal up-right arrow. Services: a white open hand with an arrow pointing right, drawn as a flat silhouette. Execution: a white target (concentric rings) with an arrow hitting the centre. All icons are solid white on dark fills, with no shadow or gradient. A 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at the bottom right corner (x 88.7, y 86.7, w 7.5, h 13.3) but its content is not visible in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Flat white background with no gradient. Four column panels are dark navy #1A4366 and the Execution chevron is medium teal #2380A3; a white band separates the panels from the teal shape. Title text is navy #1A4366, bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle is navy #1A4366, regular Arial, about 3.7% of slide height. Column headings are white Arial Bold about 4.4% of slide height. Captions are white Arial Bold about 2.9% of slide height. Heading boxes are thin white rectangular outlines with square corners, no fill and no shadow. Panels have square corners and no shadows. The Company Confidential line is grey Arial Italic, and page numbers are Arial in small size.

### Slide 16

**Text**

ADI has multiple levers to deliver above market organic / growth while expanding AEBITDA margins / $4.8B / REVENUE / 6.2% / Standalone / Adj. EBITDA / MARGIN¹ % / CATEGORY / LEADERSHIP / OMNICHANNEL / EXPERIENCE / EXCLUSIVE / BRANDS / EXPANSION / CATEGORIES / VALUE-ADDED / SERVICES / EXECUTION / EXCELLENCE / ~$6B / REVENUE / >8% / Standalone / Adj. EBITDA / MARGIN¹ % / 2025A / 2030 Target / 1 Standalone Adj. EBITDA is Adjusted EBITDA less standalone adjustments. Standalone Adjusted EBITDA is a non-GAAP financial measure, see reconciliation in the Appendix. / Note: Forward-looking target. Results may vary, see slide 2 for more information. / 16

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a left-aligned two-line bold title at the top-left (left margin about 3.9% of width). Below it, eight vertical columns of increasing height sit side by side across the slide from x 3.3% to 96.6%, rising left to right, with their tops stepped up beneath a teal arrow that sweeps from bottom-left to top-right. Column bottoms align on a common baseline at about 84% of slide height, with 2025A and 2030 Target labels beneath the first and last columns, and two grey footnote lines plus the page number 16 at the bottom.

**Visuals**

Eight flat vertical columns in graded blue-grey colours from pale #CBD4DE at left to dark slate #2C4051 at right, each with a sloped top cap, with white icons and white bold labels in the middle columns. A teal (#5BD3D0 to #2380A3 gradient) curved arrow rises from a solid teal dot at the left (about x 7.5% y 49.5%) to an arrowhead at the top right (about x 89% y 18.5%), passing over the tops of the columns. Icons in white, all approximately: category leadership podium with two figures and a raised arm (about x 18.9 y 61.0 w 3.4 h 5.3); omnichannel mobile phone showing a shopping cart (about x 31.3 y 61.2 w 2.1 h 6.1); exclusive brands cycle arrows around a box (about x 42.3 y 61.3 w 3.4 h 5.5); expansion circle with diagonal up-right arrow (about x 54.1 y 61.3 w 3.2 h 5.6); value-added services open hand holding a flat object (about x 65.7 y 62.7 w 3.6 h 2.8); execution excellence target with arrow (about x 77.5 y 61.3 w 3.2 h 5.5). No photographs. No charts. Two placed images are in the file: a 830 by 194 pixel image covering the arrow area (x 7.5 y 18.5 w 86.5 h 35.9) and a 220 by 220 pixel image at x 88.7 y 86.7 w 7.5 h 13.3 that shows no discernible content on the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF. Title text dark navy #1A4366, Arial Bold (sans-serif), approx 5.6% of slide height per line, so about 11% of height for the two-line title, left aligned. Body and value text navy #1A4366 on light columns and white #FFFFFF on dark columns. Values such as $4.8B and ~$6B are Arial Bold at about 5.2% of slide height; labels such as REVENUE are Arial Regular at about 1.9%; bar labels are Arial Bold capitals at about 2.3%, white. Year labels 2025A and 2030 Target are Arial Regular about 2.9%, navy. Footnotes are Arial Regular about 1.7%, grey #7F7F7F. Accent colours: teal #2380A3 and bright teal #5BD3D0 on the arrow and dot; navy #1A4366 accent; slate #2C4051 for the final column and page number. Shapes are rectangles with square corners and no shadows, separated by thin white gaps of about 0.5% width. The arrow is a thick tapered stroke with a triangular head. No gradient fills on the columns.

**Layout**

Two-column split layout on a full-bleed white slide. The bold two-line title sits top-left, left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge, above a full-width teal header band. Below the band, a light grey left column (about 51% wide) meets a dark navy right column whose left edge is a chevron notch pointing right; each column holds four icon-and-text rows, left-aligned with icons at the left of each row. The footer 'Company Confidential' sits bottom-left and the slide number '17' sits bottom-right inside the navy panel.

**Visuals**

Eight small line icons in two columns of four. Left column (on light grey): four check-mark-in-circle icons, navy #1A4366 outline, about 3% of slide width each, one aligned with each left bullet row (rows at about 40%, 54%, 68% and 81% of slide height). Right column (on navy): four plus-in-circle icons, white outline, about 3% of slide width each, one aligned with each right row (rows at about 40%, 55%, 69% and 82% of slide height). Two embedded pictures are present in the file at the bottom-right corner (a 220 by 220 pixel image at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, 7.5% by 13.3%, and a 145 by 60 pixel image at x 91.7%, y 91.2%, 5.0% by 3.6%); no visible logo or artwork appears in the rendered slide, so their content cannot be identified. No charts, tables or photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White full-bleed background. Teal header band #2380A3 across full width. Left panel light grey #E5E9EF, right panel navy #1A4366 with a chevron-shaped left edge that points right, its tip near the vertical middle at about 51% of slide width. Title in bold Arial (or Helvetica-equivalent) sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height per line. Header labels and body text in regular Arial sans-serif, about 3.7% of slide height; header labels and right-column text white #FFFFFF; left-column body text navy #1A4366. Footer in italic Arial grey #7F7F7F, about 1.9% of slide height. Shapes are flat with square corners, no shadows or gradients, and no visible outlines.

**Layout**

Three circles in one horizontal row, centred vertically in the middle band of the slide, with a small plus badge between circles 1 and 2 and a small equals badge between circles 2 and 3. The title sits top-left, left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge, in two lines spanning the top fifth. Footnotes sit bottom-left at about 94% to 97% of slide height; the ADI logo sits bottom-right and the page number 18 sits at the bottom-right corner, right-aligned, both inside the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Three filled circles and two small round badges, no photographs or charts. Circle 1 (left, light grey-blue #CBD4DE) contains the heading 'In-year Focus' and three lines: 'Org alignment', 'Continued SNPO synergies', 'Store footprint'. Circle 2 (middle, #CBD4DE) contains 'Near-term Focus' and three lines: 'Real estate footprint', 'Platform consolidation', 'AI / Automation'. Circle 3 (right, dark navy #1A4366, largest visual weight) contains '$80M+' in large bold white and the italic white caption 'Run-rate operating savings targeted to be achieved by end of 2027' with superscript 1. Between circles 1 and 2 is a navy #1A4366 circle with a white plus sign, about 5.2% wide. Between circles 2 and 3 is a teal #2380A3 circle with a white equals sign, about 5.2% wide. Circles are the same size (about 28% of slide width, 50% of slide height) and in a row with equal gaps. The ADI logo is navy #1A4366 bold stylised wordmark at bottom-right.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title text in bold Arial-style sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height, two lines with tight leading. Body text in regular Arial-style sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 3.3% of slide height (heading inside circles bold, about 4.4%). Circle 3 text is white. Accent colours: navy #1A4366 and teal #2380A3, light grey-blue #CBD4DE for circles 1 and 2. Footnotes in grey #7E7E7E, about 1.7% of slide height, regular sans-serif. Page number in dark slate #2C4051, about 1.9%. Shapes are flat solid circles with no outlines, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded rectangles.

The title sits top-left, left-aligned at x 3.9%, spanning two lines at the top of the slide with the baseline of the second line near y 18%. Column headers sit in a row at y 19.9% to 29.6%, aligned with the top of the grid. Left margin about 3.2%, right margin about 3%. Footnotes are left-aligned at x 3.9% in the bottom band from y 91.7% to 98%, the ADI logo sits bottom-right at x 91.7%, and the page number '19' sits bottom-right at x 97.7%, y 96.5%.

Table-style comparison graphic, not a chart. **Layout**

Two-zone slide: a light grey-blue left panel (about 0 to 80.6% of the width, full height) holds the title, a 4 by 2 grid of circular headshots with name, job title and tenure labels, and a footnote; a white right column (80.6 to 100%) holds the 'Prior Experience' heading and a vertical stack of ten logos. The two-line title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and 4% from the left edge, left-aligned. Headshot rows start at about 19.5% and 57.1% from the top, with columns centred at about 10.6%, 30.4%, 50.3% and 70.2% of the width. The 'Years of Industry Experience' label box sits bottom-centre of the grey panel, the footnote sits bottom-left, and the page number '20' sits bottom-right in the white column.

**Visuals**

Eight circular headshot photographs in a 4 by 2 grid, each cropped to a circle about 12.7% of the slide width and 22.5% of its height, with no overlay. Top row, left to right: Rob Aarnes, a smiling man with short brown hair in a navy blazer over a light blue open-collar shirt against a blurred bright office background; Leigh Ann Angelini, a smiling woman with long brown hair in a black and white striped top against a blurred light background; Mike Carlet, a man with short dark hair and a grey-white beard in a white open-collar shirt against a dark brown blurred background; Allie Copeland, a smiling woman with long dark wavy hair in a dark top against a blurred light office background. Bottom row, left to right: Marco Cardazzi, a smiling man in a navy suit and open-collar shirt against a blurred background with a green plant at the right edge; Jeannine Lane, a smiling woman with curly dark hair and a gold necklace in a dark top against a blurred light background; James Olender, a smiling man with short hair and dark-rimmed glasses in a dark jacket over a plaid shirt against a blurred grey background; Stu Tisdale, a smiling man with short dark hair in a blue checked blazer and light blue shirt against a blurred grey-brown background. Lighting across all portraits is soft and even, with dominant colours of navy, white, grey-blue and warm skin tones. Right column: ten logos stacked top to bottom: Driven Brands (red and yellow wordmark with a red swoosh, about 18.0 to 23.9% from the top), GE Vernova (green GE monogram and green spaced capitals), Global Industrial (red and blue mark with blue capitals), Henry Schein (dark grey capitals with a red and blue flag mark), Honeywell (red wordmark), MSC (red letters with the tagline 'BUILT TO MAKE YOU BETTER' in dark grey), PPG (blue PPG square mark with grey tagline 'We protect and beautify the world'), Rexel (white letters on a dark blue block with a lighter blue bar beneath), Resideo (black lowercase wordmark), Snap One (dark grey and orange mark with black wordmark). A separate square image of 220 by 220 pixels sits at about 88.7% from the left and 86.7% from the top with no identifiable visible content. Below the logos, a small white rectangular label box with the text 'Years of Industry Experience' and a superscript 1. Eight small white rectangular boxes, each about 8 to 12% of the slide width and 2 to 2.5% of its height, sit under each person's title and display the tenure text.

**Design**

Background is white with a light grey-blue panel #E5E9EF covering the left 80.6% of the slide. Titles and names are dark navy blue #1A4366; body and tenure text is #1A4366 as well; footnote text is grey #7E7E7E; the confidentiality line is grey #7F7F7F; the page number is slate #2C4051. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif): bold for the title and personal names, regular for job titles, tenure labels and the 'Prior Experience' heading. The title is about 5.6% of slide height, names about 2.5%, job titles about 2.3%, tenure labels and the section label about 1.9%, the footnote about 1.7%. Tenure labels sit in small white boxes with no visible border and no shadow. The white 'Years of Industry Experience' box has about 85% opacity. No gradients, no corner radii, no shadows.

### Slide 21

**Text**

We are bolstered by a Board of Directors with strong / domain expertise / Michael Kaufmann / Chairman of ADIG Board / Former CEO, Cardinal Health / Rob Aarnes / President & CEO, ADI / William Galvin / Former CEO, Anixter / Christine Gorjanc / Former CFO, / Arlo & NETGEAR / Cynthia Hostetler / Former President, / First Manhattan Bancorporation / Stephen LeClair / Former CEO & Chairman, / Core & Main / Nathan K. Sleeper / CEO, CD&R / Brian Walker / SVP Sales & Onsite Services, / W.W. Grainger / Committee Chairs: / A / Audit / C / Compensation / N / Nominating & Governance / R / Transitioning from Resideo Board of Directors / Company Confidential / 21

**Layout**

Title is left-aligned at the top, starting at about 3.9% from the left edge and spanning two lines. Below it, eight circular headshots sit in a four-column by two-row grid, each column centred under its photo with a bold name and one or two centred role lines beneath. A bottom strip holds two translucent white legend panels side by side, with the ADI logo at bottom right, a grey italic 'Company Confidential' label at bottom left and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eight circular headshot photographs, each about 12.7% wide and 22.5% tall, arranged in two rows. Row 1 (top at y 20.5%): Michael Kaufmann, Rob Aarnes, William Galvin, Christine Gorjanc, left to right. Row 2 (top at y 55.5%): Cynthia Hostetler, Stephen LeClair, Nathan K. Sleeper, Brian Walker, left to right. Each photo is a head-and-shoulders portrait of a smiling person, in full colour, with no border or overlay. Michael Kaufmann: older man with grey hair, navy suit, light blue shirt, blue patterned tie, grey-blue background. Rob Aarnes: man with short brown hair, navy jacket, light blue open-collar shirt, blurred warm beige background. William Galvin: man with grey-brown hair, brown blazer, open white collar, warm ochre background. Christine Gorjanc: woman with shoulder-length auburn hair, black-framed glasses, red scarf, black jacket, near-black background. Cynthia Hostetler: woman with long blonde hair, teal top, warm brown blurred background. Stephen LeClair: man with short grey-brown hair, navy jacket, light blue open shirt, pale grey blurred background. Nathan K. Sleeper: man with dark hair, dark suit, light blue shirt, red tie, mid-grey background. Brian Walker: man with short grey hair, dark-rimmed glasses, navy jacket, light blue shirt, blurred green foliage background. Lighting is soft and even on all portraits. Committee badges: outlined circles with a navy letter, drawn with a thin navy stroke and no fill: A on Christine Gorjanc at lower right of her photo; R and N side by side at lower right of Cynthia Hostetler's photo; C at lower right of Stephen LeClair's photo; R at lower right of Nathan K. Sleeper's photo. Legend strip: two white rounded-free rectangles at 77% opacity. The left panel contains the bold label 'Committee Chairs:' followed by outlined badges A (Audit), C (Compensation) and N (Nominating & Governance). The right panel contains badge R followed by 'Transitioning from Resideo Board of Directors'. ADI logo: bold navy wordmark 'ADI' with slanted letterforms at bottom right. One additional image of 220 by 220 pixels sits at the bottom right corner (x 88.7%, y 86.7%) and shows no visible content against the background.

**Design**

Background: Solid light grey-blue #E5E9EF, full bleed. Light grey-blue background #E5E9EF with flat colour and no gradients or textures. Title, names, role lines, badge letters and legend text are navy #1A4366. The footer label is grey #7F7F7F and the page number is slate #2C4051. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like) sans-serif: titles and names in Arial Bold, role lines and legend text in Arial Regular. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, names about 2.5%, role lines and body about 2.0%, legend about 1.9%. Legend panels are flat white at 77% opacity with square corners and no shadow. Photos are plain circles with no outline or shadow. Badges are thin navy circle outlines with no fill.

### Slide 22

**Text**

ADI’s next stage of growth is here
1 / 2 / 3 / 4
Category- / leading / Positions
Preeminent / Omnichannel / One-stop Shop
Multiple Levers / for Above / Market Growth
Attractive / Margin Upside / Potential & Cash / Flow
Company Confidential
22

**Layout**

Single-row layout of four equal rounded cards spanning the slide width, with a left-aligned title at the top-left (x 3.9%, y 6.1%) and the cards centred in the middle band (y 26.8% to 80.1%). Side margins are about 5.3% and gutters about 1.8%. The ADI logo sits bottom right at x 88.7% and the page number 22 sits at the far bottom right (x 97.7%, y 96.5%), with the 'Company Confidential' line bottom left (x 1.6%, y 95.8%).

**Visuals**

Four identical solid navy rounded rectangles (fill #1A4366, corner radius about 70 px on a 2000 px wide render, roughly one sixth of the card width, no shadow, no border). Each card carries one thin outline circle in teal #66D7D1 (stroke about 2 px, diameter about 5.9% of slide width) centred horizontally near the upper third of the card, containing a light-weight sans-serif numeral: 1, 2, 3, 4 in teal #66D7D1, left to right. Below each circle is a centred white bold label. No photographs, charts or icons other than the circles and the ADI logo. The logo is a bold navy #1A4366 'ADI' wordmark with a slanted A, placed as two overlapping images (a 220 by 220 px image at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, w 7.5%, h 13.3%, and a 145 by 60 px image at x 91.7%, y 91.2%, w 5.0%, h 3.6%) and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or pattern. Title in bold Arial-style grotesque sans-serif (Arial Bold), navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height, left aligned. Card labels in Arial Bold, white #FFFFFF, about 3.7% of slide height, centred, with tight leading (about 4.4% line pitch). Accent teal #66D7D1 for the circle outlines and numerals. Cards are navy #1A4366 with large rounded corners and no shadow. Footer text in Arial Italic grey #7F7F7F, page number in Arial regular dark slate #2C4051 at about 1.9% of slide height.

### Slide 23

**Text**

Products and Solutions for Pros
Be the catalyst
for growth for
Pros and suppliers
Marco Cardazzi
SVP, Chief Merchandising Officer
Company Confidential
23

**Layout**

Single title slide with a left-aligned text block starting at about 6.9% from the left edge, sitting in the lower-middle of the slide. The stack runs from a teal subtitle, through a large three-line bold white title, down to the presenter name and role near the bottom. The ADI logo sits bottom right, and the page number sits in the bottom-right corner. A small 'Company Confidential' line sits bottom left. There is no header or content grid.

**Visuals**

One full-bleed background illustration: a repeating line-art pattern of interlocking open triangles and chevrons, drawn in thin darker-blue strokes over the gradient, running diagonally from upper left to lower right. One ADI logo at bottom right: white stylised wordmark with slanted letterforms, mostly solid white with thin diagonal cut-outs. A second 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at x 88.7, y 86.7 and is not visibly distinguishable on the render, so it appears transparent or blends into the background. No photographs, charts or tables.

**Design**

Background is a dark-to-mid blue gradient running from deep navy at the lower left to brighter cerulean at the upper right, with a subtle tonal line pattern over it. Title text is white #FFFFFF in bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 7.5% of slide height per line with tight leading. Subtitle is teal #66D7D1 in regular Arial-style sans-serif at about 4.1% of slide height. Presenter name is bold white at about 4.4% and role is regular white at about 3.3%. The confidentiality line is grey #7F7F7F italic at about 1.9%. Page number is white at about 1.9%. No shapes, rules, corner radii or shadows.

**Layout**

Single-row composition on a white field: a bold title centred across the top, six equal portrait photos in one horizontal row across the middle, three labels above and three below the row joined to each photo by a vertical connector line with dot ends, a dashed arrow with a closing centred statement at the bottom, and the page number at bottom right. The title sits at about 6% from the top and the closing line at about 89%. Left and right margins are roughly 10%. A small grey confidential mark sits bottom left but is not visible in the rendered view.

**Visuals**

Six photographs in a row, each 330 by 440 pixels, shown as equal portrait rectangles with square corners and no overlay. Photo 1 (left, x 10.4): a middle-aged man with glasses and short grey hair in a light blue shirt with rolled sleeves, holding a phone to his ear, seated near a warm lamp in a bright interior; warm beige and cream tones; cropped to a tall rectangle. Photo 2 (x 23.6): two men in conversation in front of blue and orange balloons; one older man in a dark grey jacket seen from behind, the other in a navy polo shirt with a small logo, gesturing with open hands; warm daylight. Photo 3 (x 36.8): a man with short hair and stubble in a white button shirt typing on a silver laptop on a wooden table, a black phone beside the laptop; soft natural light; cream and brown tones. Photo 4 (x 50.1): a man with glasses in a blue shirt seen from behind and side, hand at chin, looking at a monitor showing an ADI dashboard with a line chart and tables; blurred glass wall with handwritten notes behind; blue and grey tones. Photo 5 (x 63.4): a smiling man in a navy polo at a service counter handing items to a customer in a grey jacket and cap; cardboard boxes on the counter, a wooden pillar and a wall chart behind; warm interior light. Photo 6 (x 76.6, right): a man seen from behind and above, in a dark top, typing on a silver laptop showing a web app with a red accent; light grey blurred background; cool neutral tones. Connector lines: six thin blue vertical lines, each ending in a filled dot at both ends, running from a label down to or up to the top or bottom edge of its photo or the row of photos. A dashed blue horizontal arrow runs across the lower part of the slide, from about x 10.6 to x 87.9, with a solid filled arrowhead at the right end, dashes about 1 percent of the slide width long. A small image of 220 by 220 pixels is placed at x 88.7, y 86.7, w 7.5, h 13.3 but shows nothing visible in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, pattern or texture. Titles and labels are bold Arial (Helvetica-like), sans-serif, in dark blue #1A4366. Title cap height is about 5.6 percent of slide height, labels and the closing line are about 2.9 and 3.7 percent. Body labels are set in tight two-line blocks. Accent colour is teal blue #2380A3 for connector lines, dots and the dashed arrow. Connector lines are about 2 pixels wide with dot ends about 1 percent of slide width in diameter. Photos have square corners, no border and no shadow. Page number and confidential mark are small grey Arial, about 1.9 percent of slide height.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck states one four-part case on slide 6 and carries it consistently through the section dividers, the top navigation tabs (slides 25 to 54) and the close on slide 65, so the audience can follow the argument across 69 pages.

**Text**

Category Leadership
Omnichannel Experience
Exclusive Brands
Expansion Categories
Value-added Services
Execution Excellence
ADI accelerates the last mile for Pros & suppliers
Manufacturers
(Suppliers)
ADI for Suppliers
One partner and ~20 shipping locations gives you
access to ~100k customers
• ~1.7k sales associates as extension of GTM
• Customer proximity across ~200 stores
1
• Marketing + analytics platform to grow
Integrators
(Pros)
ADI for Pros
One partner who understands your business,
product needs, and can support you
• Omnichannel, one-stop shop for products
• Valued manufacturer of Exclusive Brands
• Support + services to make the job easier
Security
AV
Fire
DataCom
Company Confidential
25
1 ~200 stores represents store count as of FY 2025; this number is expected to be optimized with shared-market store combinations for a target count around 160 in current footprint

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation bar runs across the top edge (y 0 to 4), with the first tab filled dark navy and the other five light grey. The title sits top-left at x 3.9, y 6.1 across roughly three-quarters of the width, left-aligned. The main area is two columns: a pyramid diagram on the left (Manufacturers box at top, ADI box in the middle inside a dashed outline, Integrators box at bottom with four sub-boxes) and two stacked text sections on the right (ADI for Suppliers at upper right, ADI for Pros at lower right), each with a circular arrow icon at its left edge. A footnote sits bottom-left at y 96.4 and the page number 25 sits bottom-right at x 97.7, y 96.5.

**Visuals**

Shapes: a light grey #D9D9D9 pyramid (a tall isosceles triangle with apex near x 26.8, y 17.2, widening to a base spanning x 5.3 to 48.4 at the bottom of the diagram, partly hidden behind the boxes). Three stacked boxes form the pyramid: Manufacturers (Suppliers) in #2380A3 at top, ADI in #1A4366 in the middle, Integrators (Pros) in #2C4051 at bottom with four light grey-white sub-boxes labelled Security, AV, Fire and DataCom. A dashed navy outline (#1A4366) surrounds the ADI box with a gap of about 1 percent of width on each side. Two block arrows connect the boxes: a short #2380A3 downward arrow from Manufacturers to ADI, and a #1A4366 downward arrow from the ADI box (through the dashed outline) to Integrators. Logo: the white ADI wordmark centred in the ADI box, bold sans-serif capitals, no tagline. Two circular icons, each a filled circle with a white right-pointing arrow: the upper one #2380A3 beside ADI for Suppliers, the lower one #2C4051 beside ADI for Pros. Tab icons, one per tab, in pale grey-white line style: a group of figures with bars (Category Leadership), a phone (Omnichannel Experience), a box with cycling arrows (Exclusive Brands), an arrow pointing up-right in a circle (Expansion Categories), a handshake (Value-added Services), a concentric target (Execution Excellence).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Flat white background with no gradients, shadows or textures. Titles and headers: navy #1A4366 for the slide title and the dark tab; white on the blue and navy header bars. Body text: dark slate #2C4051. Accents: teal-blue #2380A3 for the Manufacturers box, the suppliers header and arrows; slate #2C4051 for Integrators and the pros header; grey #D9D9D9 for the pyramid; #7F8C97-like grey sub-boxes at about 41 percent white opacity over the slate box. Tab labels are #CBD4DE on #E5E9EF pills, except Category Leadership which is white bold on navy. Typeface: Arial (ArialMT regular, Arial-BoldMT bold, Arial-ItalicMT for the confidentiality mark), a neo-grotesque sans-serif. Title is bold at about 5.6 percent of slide height (cap height about 4 percent); body text about 3.3 percent for headings and about 3.2 percent for bullets; footnote 1.7 percent; tab labels 1.9 percent. Rectangles have square corners with no outlines except the dashed navy frame around ADI (about 0.3 percent stroke width, dash length about 1 percent). Arrows are solid block arrows with square shafts.

26

**Layout**

A white slide with a row of six section tabs across the top edge (the first, Category Leadership, is active), then a large left-aligned bold title at about 6% from the top, then a 3-column by 3-row grid of nine equal category panels (each about 30% of slide width, with about 2.6% and 35% and 67.6% left edges) with grey header bars and logo rows beneath each header. Footer note sits bottom-left at about 96% height, with the page number 26 at bottom-right, and a small italic 'Company Confidential' mark at bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Six small line icons sit in the top tab bar, one at the left of each tab label: a people/podium figure (Category Leadership, white on navy), a phone/device outline, a box with arrow, an upward arrow in a circle, a handshake/plane shape, and a target with arrow (the last five icons are light grey #CBD4DE). No photographs or charts. Nine category panels contain brand logos in two rows each, about 80 logos in total, shown as dark and coloured wordmarks on white: Video Surveillance (AXIS COMMUNICATIONS, DW orange, exacq, Hanwha Techwin America, Honeywell red; LUMA SURVEILLANCE, PELCO, speco technologies, TURING, VIVOTEK); Fire / Life Safety (EATON blue, FIRE-LITE ALARMS by Honeywell, GENTEX CORPORATION black, Kidde red, NAPCO SECURITY TECHNOLOGIES red; POTTER red, STI Safety Technology International red circle, SDI blue swoosh, Honeywell red, SYSTEM SENSOR blue); Networking (araknis NETWORKS green, D-Link FOR BUSINESS blue, eero black, LANTRONIX grey, LUXUL black; NETGEAR black, TRENDnet blue, veracity grey, VIGITRON red and black, UBIQUITI black); Intrusion & Smart Home (2GIG blue cube, resideo black, ALARM.COM, Google multicolour, DSC red; Qolsys black, Kwikset red, NAPCO SECURITY TECHNOLOGIES red, ring grey, Yale yellow circle); Access Control & Communications (2N blue square, AIPHONE blue, Altronix red, CDVI blue, dormakaba blue; hes ASSA ABLOY green, HID blue box, Honeywell red, Paxton green triangle, SECURITRON ASSA ABLOY black); Wire & Cable (ADI PRO red, Belden purple, HUBBELL yellow outline, LEVITON green square, ORTRONICS green; PROTERIAL navy, prysmian teal, SIEMON red, Southwire brown, SUMITOMO ELECTRIC LIGHTWAVE navy); Professional AV (AtlasIED blue, BOSE PROFESSIONAL black, JBL PROFESSIONAL red square, legrand AV red, LG Business Solutions purple; logitech black, NETGEAR black, Q-SYS blue, SAMSUNG blue, SONY black); Residential AV (Control4 grey, episode red, DENON black, KEF black, Klipsch black; SAMSUNG blue, SANUS white on black, SONOS black, SONY black, STRONG black outlined box); Data Communications (APC red, comnet blue, EATON blue, FLUKE networks blue, MINUTEMAN navy; SUMITOMO ELECTRIC LIGHTWAVE navy, TREND NETWORKS blue, TRIPP LITE blue, VERTIV black, WILSON ELECTRONICS blue). Logos are flat, unframed, centred in their rows and not cropped.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with navy #1A4366 titles and category headers on light grey #E5E9EF header bars with square corners and no shadows. The top tab bar uses rounded rectangles with a navy #1A4366 active tab and light grey #E5E9EF inactive tabs with pale grey #CBD4DE labels. Title is bold Arial (Helvetica-style grotesque sans-serif), about 5.6% of slide height, navy #1A4366. Category headers are bold Arial about 2.9% of slide height, navy #1A4366, centred. Footer note is regular Arial about 1.7% of height in grey #7E7E7E; 'Company Confidential' is italic Arial in #7F7F7F; the page number is regular Arial in #2C4051. Body logos are small, roughly 2.5 to 5% of slide width each, with no outlines or shadows.

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge (y 0 to 4%), with the first tab highlighted navy and the other five in light grey. The title sits left-aligned at about 4% from the left edge, starting at y 6.1%. Below, a light grey left panel holds a 2 by 3 grid of six boxes with a navy header bar above, while a navy chevron-edged right panel holds the ADI Value Creation header and three numbered points stacked vertically with equal spacing. The page number sits bottom right at x 97.7%, and the Company Confidential line sits bottom left.

**Visuals**

Six small line icons in the top tab strip, one per tab, drawn in light grey on grey tabs and white on the navy tab: a group of three people for Category Leadership, a phone for Omnichannel Experience, a box or cube for Exclusive Brands, a circular arrow with an outward arrow for Expansion Categories, a hand offering a document or service for Value-added Services, and a concentric target for Execution Excellence. Six-cell grid on the left, each cell an outlined rectangle with a grey-blue stroke of about 2 px and a light grey fill, with two navy circles with white plus signs overlapping the grid intersections at about x 25.3% (y 51.1% and y 69.6%). Right side has a navy chevron panel whose left edge is an inward-pointing V with its point at about x 63.4%, y 51%. Three white-outlined circles of about 4% width each contain the numerals 1, 2 and 3 in bold white. An embedded 220 by 220 pixel image sits at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, w 7.5%, h 13.3%, over the navy panel; it is not legible as a distinct picture on the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with light grey #E5E9EF left panel and navy #1A4366 right panel placed on top. Clean corporate style. White slide background, light grey #E5E9EF left panel, navy #1A4366 right panel and header bar. Title text navy #1A4366, bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), about 5.6% of slide height. Body text in grid cells navy #1A4366 in regular Arial, about 3.7% of slide height. Right-hand points white #FFFFFF regular Arial about 3.7%. Numerals bold Arial in white, about 4.4%. Header text bold Arial about 3.7%, white on navy and navy on white. Tab labels bold Arial about 1.9% on the active tab in white, and regular Arial about 1.9% in #CBD4DE on inactive tabs. Tabs are rounded rectangles with small corner radii, no shadows, no gradients, no visible outlines.

28

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge, with the fourth tab (Expansion Categories) highlighted as active. The title sits top-left below the tabs at about 6% from the top and spans most of the width. A full-width teal band carries a two-part subtitle (left-aligned start and right-aligned end) at about 19% to 27% of height. Below it, a left column holds the bar chart (about 4% to 51% width) with its title above, and a dark right panel (about 70% to 100% width, 27% to 100% height) holds two stacked statistics. A circled 43% CAGR callout sits between chart and panel. Footnotes and source are bottom-left at about 94% to 97% height, and the page number sits bottom-right inside the dark panel.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical column chart titled ADI Expansion Category Revenue, FY 2018 - 2025. Y axis has no printed scale or units; no axis labels are printed. X axis: categories 2018 (superscript 1), 2019 (superscript 1), 2020 (superscript 1), 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 (superscript 2), 2025 (superscript 2). Two stacked series: navy #1A4366 (bottom) and grey-blue #7C93AD (top); no legend printed. Printed values: about $50M labelled on the 2018 column and about $600M labelled above the 2025 column; no other values printed. Bar heights in the file, as share of the 2025 total column (total top = 39.3% of slide height, base = 85.8%): 2018 navy about 3.8% tall (no grey); 2019 navy about 4.3% (no grey); 2020 navy about 9.4% (no grey); 2021 navy 12.8% and grey about 8.4% on top; 2022 navy 14.8% and grey 13.1%; 2023 navy 17.1% and grey 13.6%; 2024 navy 22.9% and grey 14.9%; 2025 navy 29.6% and grey 16.9%. Highlighted points: none; the 2025 column and the ~$600M label are the emphasised end point. Callout: white circle with thin navy #1A4366 outline containing 43% growth CAGR 2018 - 2025. Right panel: no chart, two statistics. Baseline: thin dark navy horizontal axis line under the columns. Tab icons: small grey line icons (podium/bars, phone, cube box, handshake, target) on the inactive tabs; a white circled up-right arrow on the active tab. A placed 220 by 220 px image lies at the bottom of the dark panel but is not visibly rendered.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF background. Clean corporate sans-serif look (Arial/Helvetica style) on a white background. Title in bold navy #1A4366, body and labels in regular navy #1A4366 or white on dark. Accents: teal band #2380A3, slate panel #2C4051, bar greys #7C93AD and navy #1A4366. Title is large (about 5.6% of slide height); subtitle text about 3.7%; chart labels about 2.5% to 3.0%; footnotes about 1.7% to 1.9%. Tabs are light grey #E5E9EF pills with rounded bottom corners, flat with no shadow; the active tab is navy #1A4366. Thin navy outline on the circle callout. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 29

**Review notes**

- Review: Action titles say the point, the navy and teal palette is applied consistently, and the data slides are readable: slide 9 pairs a clean bar chart with four large KPI callouts, and slide 29 gives margin context under each bar.
- Worth keeping: Keep the consistent title-plus-callout-panel layout, the numbered four-pillar framing on slide 6, the chart treatments on slides 9 and 29, and the section tabs and dividers that orient the audience.

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / Exclusive Brands are a profitable and / durable advantage for ADI / ADI Exclusive Brands (EB) / Revenue FY 2018 - 2025 / >$0.8B / <$0.1B / 3x / Gross margin rate / of EB compared to 3P² / 18% / % of total revenue in 2025 / compared to <3% in 2018 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024¹ / 2025¹ / EB product line / margin contrib. / (% total) / <5% / 6% / 6% / 7% / 8% / 9% / 32% / 42% / 1 Results reflect Snap One acquisition beginning June 2024 / 2 Gross margin rate of Exclusive Brands versus third party products observed at 3x for FY2025; 3P = Third Party Products / Source: Company Management Analysis / 29

**Layout**

Two columns: a white left area of about 70% width holds a six-tab navigation bar across the top, a two-line bold title at top left, the subtitle and bar chart, a grey margin-contribution band with a row of eight ovals, and footnotes at the bottom; a full-height dark navy panel fills the right 30% and holds three stacked key figures centred on the panel. The title sits top-left at x 3.9% with a left margin of about 3.9% and no visible right margin issues; the footnotes and source line are bottom-left at x 3.5%, and the page number 29 sits bottom-right inside the panel at x 97.7%.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical bar chart titled ADI Exclusive Brands (EB) Revenue FY 2018 - 2025 (subtitle), categories 2018 to 2025 on the horizontal axis (2024 and 2025 marked with superscript 1 referring to footnote 1), no visible value axis, no gridlines, no axis units printed. Eight bars in one series, flat fill #2380A3, all bars about 3.5% of slide width wide with equal spacing; printed values only at the ends: 2018 labelled <$0.1B and 2025 labelled >$0.8B. Unlabelled values are estimates: 2019 about $0.08B, 2020 about $0.07B, 2021 about $0.10B, 2022 about $0.12B, 2023 about $0.13B, 2024 about $0.5B (bar height 23.9% of slide versus 38.3% for 2025). No legend. Highlighted points: none beyond the printed end labels. Under the chart, a grey band row titled EB product line margin contrib. (% total) lists eight outlined ovals with values in order: 2018 <5%, 2019 6%, 2020 6%, 2021 7%, 2022 8%, 2023 9%, 2024 32%, 2025 42%; each oval has a teal outline and bold navy value text. Right panel: three large white figures: 3x with caption Gross margin rate of EB compared to 3P² (footnote 2 marker); 18% with caption % of total revenue in 2025 compared to <3% in 2018. Icons: six small grey-white icons in the navigation tabs: a cluster of buildings and people for Category Leadership, a mobile phone for Omnichannel Experience, a box with circular arrows in white for Exclusive Brands, a circle with an arrow for Expansion Categories, a handshake for Value-added Services, and a target with concentric rings for Execution Excellence. A placed raster image (220 by 220 px) sits at the bottom right over the dark panel and is not visible in the render. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the left, with a dark navy #2C4051 full-height panel covering the right 30.1% of the slide. Clean corporate consulting style. Background white #FFFFFF on the left with a full-height dark navy #2C4051 panel on the right 30%. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold or Helvetica Bold) in navy #1A4366, two lines, about 5.6% of slide height, left aligned. Subtitle in regular sans-serif (Arial) teal #2380A3 about 2.9% of slide height. Chart bars flat teal #2380A3, axis baseline navy #1A4366 about 0.2% thick. Key figures in white Arial Bold about 9% of slide height for the numerals (3x, 18%) and regular white Arial about 3.3% for captions. Printed values >$0.8B and <$0.1B in bold navy Arial about 5.2%. Year labels and row labels regular navy Arial about 2.5% and 1.9%. Footnotes grey #7E7E7E Arial about 1.7%. Navigation tabs are rounded-bottom pills, fill #E5E9EF, the active tab filled navy #1A4366 with bold white text; inactive tab text is pale grey #CBD4DE about 1.9%. Margin ovals: outline teal #2380A3 about 2 px, fill matching band #E5E9EF, no shadow. Grey band #E5E9EF full-width of the white area. No gradients, no shadows, no corner radius on the bars.
