**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge in a row, with the active tab 'Exclusive Brands' filled navy. The bold title sits left-aligned below it spanning most of the width. Three checkmark bullets sit in the left half, with a product photograph occupying the right half above a full-width teal band; a navy footer band holds four centred statistics, footnotes and source line, with the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a grouped product arrangement on a white background with a soft grey floor shadow, slightly angled. From left to right: a grey round in-ceiling loudspeaker with red accents and a 'signalyze' style label, overlapping in front of it a black smartphone showing a 'Routines' home app screen, a black wall tablet showing a 'Newton Home' dashboard (security, lighting, thermostat at 72, Apple Music, camera thumbnails), a white dome security camera with a 'LUMA' logo at top, a black rack-mounted enclosure, a black WATTBOX power strip with eight outlets and 'OvrC' branding, and a grey adjustable wall-mount bracket at far right. Lighting is soft and even from above; dominant colours are white, black, charcoal grey, with the tablet's purple-blue screen and red accents. The image is not cropped, boxed without border, sits on white with no overlay. Checkmarks: three teal-blue (#2380A3) outlined circles with check ticks, roughly 3 percent of slide width each, aligned in a left column. Tab icons: small grey line icons (buildings/shelves, phone, circular arrow, arrow up-right, handshake/plane, target) in the grey tabs; white cube-with-arrows icon in the active tab. Statistic values and labels are typographic only.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF across the upper two-thirds, with dark slate #2C4051 band across the bottom third. Clean corporate style: white upper field, teal #2380A3 full-width band, dark slate #2C4051 bottom band. Title is bold Arial-style sans-serif in navy #1A4366, large (about 5.6 percent of slide height). Checkmark labels are bold navy #1A4366 at about 3.7 percent. Teal band sentence is regular Arial in white at about 3.7 percent. Statistics are bold white Arial at about 5.6 percent with labels in regular white at about 2.9 percent. Footnotes in light grey #D9D9D9 at about 1.7 percent. Tabs are rounded rectangles in #E5E9EF with grey text #CBD4DE; active tab is #1A4366 with white bold text. Checkmark circles are outlined teal strokes, no fill, no shadows.

**Layout**

Full-width white slide with a row of six navigation tabs across the top edge (the Exclusive Brands tab is the active dark tab). Below it, a left-aligned two-line title spans the top of the content area with a left margin of about 3.9% of width. Three equal columns (each 30% wide, with gaps of about 1.8%) hold a navy numbered header above a photograph, all starting at 22.7% of height and ending at about 83% of height. A full-width teal band across the bottom 12.1% carries a centred white headline; the page number sits at bottom right inside the band.

**Visuals**

Three photographs in three equal rectangular boxes below navy column headers, each with a white ring-outlined numeral above its title. Photo 1 (column 1): outdoors on a lawn with a dark brown cylindrical outdoor speaker tilted over brown wood mulch; two hands press the speaker and push its black base into the mulch; one hand wears a black smartwatch and a ring; green grass at the right edge; daylight, natural colours dominated by brown and green; tight framing, rectangular crop, boxed with no overlay. Photo 2 (column 2): a smiling middle-aged man in a grey polo shirt and grey cap holds a grey tablet in both hands, standing beside a wooden door edge at left against a bright white-grey concrete wall; a small logo on the chest of the shirt; bright diffuse daylight; dominant greys, white and warm wood brown; waist-up framing, rectangular crop, boxed with no overlay. Photo 3 (column 3): a man with glasses and a black smartwatch in a grey polo reaches up to mount a white dome-shaped device on a white suspended ceiling grid, viewed from below and behind; soft neutral light; dominant white and grey; cropped at the bottom edge, boxed with no overlay. Five small grey tab icons sit in the navigation row (a bar chart style icon, a phone style icon, a cube in a circle in white on the active tab, an arrow in a circle, a handshake, and a target), and one 220 by 220 pixel image sits at bottom right inside the teal band, not visibly rendered.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background; navy headers and title in #1A4366; teal footer band #2380A3 with white text; navigation tabs in light grey-blue #E5E9EF with grey-blue labels #CBD4DE, active tab #1A4366 with white bold label. Typeface is Arial (Arial-BoldMT for titles and headers, ArialMT for nav labels and page number, Arial-ItalicMT for the confidentiality mark), a neo-grotesque sans-serif. Title text about 5.6% of slide height, bold; column headers about 3.3% bold; numerals about 4.4% bold; band headline about 3.7% bold; nav labels about 1.9%. Shapes are square-cornered rectangles with no visible shadows or gradients. Ring numerals use thin white circular outlines.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / Our recent launches show early wins for our product strategy / with commercial applications / Business / Music Platform / OvrC Power Insights / With more commercial products in-development … / WattBox 900 / Luma NVR / Triad Stratus / …and more / Company Confidential / 32

**Layout**

A row of six rounded navigation tabs runs across the top edge, with two dark tabs (Exclusive Brands, Expansion Categories) and four pale tabs. A two-line left-aligned title sits below at about 6% from the top. Two product visuals occupy the middle: the Episode Pro Audio hardware stack on the left (about 3.5% to 62% width) and the WattBox plus OvrC logo lockup above a laptop on the right (about 61% to 95% width). A full-width navy band fills the bottom quarter, holding a centred heading and a row of four outlined boxes. The slide number sits bottom right at about 97.7% x and 96.5% y.

**Visuals**

Photograph 1 (Episode Pro Audio hardware, placed at x 3.5%, y 32.9%, 58.7% by 39.6%): a stacked arrangement of black rack-mount audio units, including amplifiers and a DSP unit with front-panel rows of terminals and vents, plus a row of white and black wall-plate controls (a volume knob plate with a small display reading '40' and 'Zone 1', a plate with RCA-style connectors, a plate with an XLR connector and a plate with a blank white face). The units are shown in three-quarter view on a white background with a soft grey floor shadow, with no crop and no overlay. Dominant colours are black, dark grey and white. Photograph 2 (laptop, placed at x 61.5%, y 31.5%, 33.5% by 39.6%): a silver laptop viewed from the front, open with the screen showing a dark-mode web dashboard (device page 'WB-800-IPVM-12' with a navigation bar, tabs and two line charts labelled Voltage and Amperage with teal traces on a dark grid). Lighting is even studio light, background white, no crop, no overlay. Logos: the Episode PRO AUDIO logo (red sine-wave mark with black lowercase 'episode' and registered mark, with 'PRO AUDIO' beneath in grey capitals) at x 12.6%, y 22.0%; the WATTBOX logo (black letters with orange 'BOX' and a lightning-bolt in the W), a black plus sign, and the blue and orange OvrC logo at x 64.4%, y 22.1%. Icons: navigation tab icons, approximately at the left of each tab: a grey podium/people icon (Category Leadership), a grey mobile-device icon (Omnichannel Experience), a white cube-with-arrows icon (Exclusive Brands), a white circle with an upward arrow (Expansion Categories), a grey aeroplane or handshake icon (Value-added Services), and a grey target icon (Execution Excellence). Four white circled plus icons, one in each of the first three bottom boxes at about x 10.5%, 31%, 51% and y 86.5% to 92%. A 220 by 220 pixel image at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, 7.5% by 13.3%, which is not visibly distinct in the render and sits within the '…and more' box region.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a full-width navy #2C4051 band across the bottom from 73.2% of height to the bottom edge. White background (#FFFFFF) with a dark navy full-width band (#2C4051) across the bottom quarter. Title text in Arial Bold, dark blue #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height per line (line spacing about 6%). Subheadings 'Business / Music Platform' and 'OvrC Power Insights' in Arial Bold, #2C4051, about 3.3% of slide height. Band heading in Arial Bold, white, about 3.7%. Box labels in Arial regular, white, about 2.9%. Navigation tab labels in Arial, about 1.9%: pale tabs use #CBD4DE text on #E5E9EF fills; active tabs use white bold text on #1A4366 fills. Tabs are rounded pill shapes with a corner radius of about half the tab height and no shadow. Bottom boxes have white 1 pt outlines, square corners, no fill. The footer confidentiality line is Arial Italic, #7F7F7F. Page number is Arial, about 1.9%, white. No gradients or shadows on the main content.

### Slide 33

**Text**

ADI has industry-leading product / depth with room to grow / 1 / Premier partner to Pros & Suppliers / 2 / Expanding assortment delivering / robust expansion category growth / 3 / Valuable Exclusive Brands are reaching / more customers with commercial NPI / 33

**Layout**

A full-height photograph fills the left 30% of the slide, and a navy panel fills the rest. The title sits top-left of the navy panel at x 35.6%, y 6.2%, in two lines. Below it, three equal horizontal rows are stacked at x 34.8% to 94.9%, each with a teal number tab on the left and a white card on the right. Text is left-aligned, and the page number sits bottom-right at about x 97.7%, y 96.5%.

**Visuals**

Photograph at left: a retail hardware store interior seen from a low angle with exposed steel roof trusses and linear ceiling lights. Hanging navy signs read Tools & Hardware, Access Control and Networking. A blue wall and a gray shutter door sit at the back. In the foreground are GENESIS cable boxes in green and yellow, pegboard hooks holding packaged hardware, and a Tools & Hardware shelf sign. The image is full-height and cropped to the left edge of the slide, with no tint or overlay. Dominant colours are blue, white and green. Lighting is bright, from overhead fluorescent fixtures. Three numbered rows: 1, 2 and 3, in white bold on teal tabs. No charts, tables, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is navy #1A4366 across the full slide. Title is white (#FFFFFF) Arial Bold sans-serif, about 5.9% of slide height. Body text in rows is teal #2380A3 Arial Bold sans-serif, about 4.4% of slide height. Numbers are white Arial Bold about 6.7% of slide height on teal tabs #2380A3. Cards are flat white #FFFFFF rectangles with no visible border, radius or shadow, and the tabs are flat teal rectangles. Footer text is Arial Italic 1.9% in gray #7F7F7F. Page number is Arial 1.9% in white. No gradients.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Omnichannel Customer Experience
Be the premier
omnichannel platform
for the industry
Stu Tisdale
SVP, Chief Digital Experience Officer
Company Confidential
34

**Layout**

Single full-bleed title slide with all text left-aligned at about 6.9% from the left edge, stacked in the lower-middle: kicker, three-line bold headline, then presenter name and role near the bottom. The ADI logo sits bottom right at about 81.8% to 94% horizontally and 83% to 92% vertically, and the page number sits in the bottom-right corner at about 97.7% horizontally. No header or content grid.

**Visuals**

One logo: the white ADI wordmark at bottom right, rendered as a bold geometric sans-serif wordmark with slanted cut strokes in the letters A and D, in #FFFFFF. One full-bleed background photograph/pattern across the whole slide: a dark-to-light blue field with a fine, tonal, repeating geometric pattern of interlocking outlined triangles and chevrons running diagonally from upper left to lower right, drawn in slightly darker blue lines at low contrast. Lighting is flat and even; the lighter area is toward the top right and the darker navy toward the bottom left. Not cropped, no framing; the pattern is an overlay on the blue gradient. No charts, tables or photographs of people.

**Design**

Background: Full-bleed blue gradient (about #1F7A9C top right to #15405F bottom left) with a subtle tonal triangle/chevron pattern. Full-bleed blue gradient background with a tonal geometric triangle/chevron pattern, lighter teal-blue at top right (about #1F7A9C) fading to deep navy at bottom left (about #15405F). Kicker 'Omnichannel Customer Experience' in teal #66D7D1, regular Arial-style sans-serif, about 4.1% of slide height, capitals only on initial letters. Headline in bold Arial-style sans-serif (Helvetica/Arial Bold), white #FFFFFF, about 7.5% of slide height, tight leading, three lines. Presenter name in bold white Arial, about 4.4% of slide height; role in regular white Arial, about 3.3% of slide height. Company Confidential note in italic grey (#7F7F7F) at about 1.9% of slide height. Page number in white Arial at about 1.9% of slide height. No rules, boxes, shadows or rounded corners.

### Slide 35

**Text**

ADI’s omnichannel platform is built to enable Pro workflows / Sales / Consultation / Order Pickup In / Store / Remotely / Manage System / Research / Online / Online Quote / Conversion / Technical / Documentation / On-The-go / ~ 75% of weekly Pro buying journeys include digital engagement, making / the digital experience operationally essential to customers / Company Confidential / 35

**Layout**

Single-row gallery on a white slide: the title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with a left margin of about 4%, and six equal-sized photos sit in one horizontal row at mid-height (about 33% to 60% of slide height), spaced roughly 13% apart and centred. Callout labels alternate above and below the photos, each joined to its photo by a thin vertical teal line ending in a dot. A dashed teal arrow runs full width near 77% of height, with a centred two-line caption beneath it; a small grey footer sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Six photographs in a row, each a plain rectangle (no border, no rounded corners, no shadow), all the same height. (1) Research Online: a laptop screen showing an e-commerce product page with a white dome security camera product in front of it, a person's hands on the keyboard, lower-left of the frame, cool grey and white tones. (2) Sales Consultation: a grey-haired man with glasses and a light blue button-up shirt, holding a phone to his ear and seated at a wooden desk beside a potted plant, warm lamp light, beige and wood tones. (3) Online Quote Conversion: an open laptop on a wooden table showing a checkout page with an order summary panel, hands on the keyboard, a plant at the right edge, warm wood and white tones. (4) Order Pickup In Store: a bearded man in a navy polo holding a stack of boxes, one labelled WG-300-IP-3, while a hand holds a rolled document in the left foreground; the left side has a grey tint, background light grey-blue. (5) Technical Documentation On-The-go: two hands holding a smartphone showing a list menu with Environmental Conditions, Documents and Resources, Frequently Bought Together, against a blurred blue-grey background. (6) Remotely Manage System: a monitor on a desk showing a dark-sidebar web dashboard with a white content panel, set against a light wood-panel wall, neutral beige tones. Each photo is shown as a boxed image, not cropped to a shape, not full-bleed. Teal connector lines (#2380A3, about 0.15% of slide width) run vertically from each label to its photo, each ending in a filled teal dot about 0.7% wide. A dashed teal arrow (#2380A3, dashes about 1% long with gaps, arrowhead at the right end) runs left to right across the slide below the photos. A 220 by 220 pixel image is recorded at x 88.7, y 86.7 but is not visibly rendered on the slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title and labels in dark navy #1A4366 Arial Bold (sans-serif), no italics, no capitals, normal letter-spacing; title about 5.6% of slide height, labels about 2.9%, bottom caption about 3.7% with bold key phrases. Accent colour teal blue #2380A3 for connector lines, dots and the dashed arrow. Footer 'Company Confidential' is grey #7F7F7F Arial Italic about 1.9%. Page number is #2C4051 Arial about 1.9%. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; the photos have square corners.

36

**Layout**

A six-tab section navigation bar runs across the top edge, with the second tab (Omnichannel Experience) highlighted in navy. The title sits left-aligned below it at about 6% from the top. Below is a two-column grid of panels headed Stores and Digital Platform, each with a photo on the left and stats and checklist on the right, followed by a full-width Distribution Network band. Notes and source sit bottom-left and the page number bottom-right, with margins of about 2.6% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Photograph left of the Stores panel: interior of an electronics retail store showing blue-and-white display kiosks with shelves of boxed products, a kiosk sign reading Hanwha Vision, a black ceiling sign reading Access Control and smaller overhead signs reading Networking and Audio / Video, grey pillars and a beige floor; full-bleed within its box, straight-on wide view, bright even overhead lighting, dominant colours blue, white, grey and beige. Photograph left of the Digital Platform panel: close-up of a hand holding a smartphone showing the ADI app (banner Stock Up and Save More, ADI REWARDS card, Top Categories list including Video Surveillance, Access Control, Residential AV, Pro AV, Fire, Intercom, Smart Home and Communications, and a View All Categories link, with a bottom navigation bar); the blurred background is blue denim jeans; shallow depth of field, soft natural light, dominant colours blue, white and dark grey; the image is cropped to a rectangle. Check-in-circle icons: nine teal outline circles with check marks, one beside each checklist item and each Distribution Network item. Small placed image 220 by 220 pixels at bottom right of the Digital Platform panel with no visible content in the render. Tab icons: small grey outline icons (buildings, phone, boxes, arrow, handshake, target) in each tab, the Omnichannel one white.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate slide on a white background. Titles and headers are bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) in navy #1A4366; the main title is about 5.6% of slide height. Stat figures are Arial Bold in teal #2380A3 at about 7.5% of slide height. Sub-labels are Arial Bold navy at about 2.9%. Checklist items are Arial regular navy #1A4366 at about 2.9%. Panel bodies are light grey #E5E9EF at 73% opacity. Accent colours are teal #2380A3 for stats and icon outlines, and navy #173D5F and #1A4366 for header bars. Shapes have square corners, except the tab bar which has rounded corners. Notes text is grey #7E7E7E at about 1.7%. No gradients or shadows.

**Layout**

A white header band across the top holds a row of six rounded section tabs (the second, Omnichannel Experience, is the active navy tab) and a bold left-aligned navy title at about 4% left margin. Below, a light grey left area holds a boxed heading over three equal text columns separated by thin vertical navy rules; a navy panel with a chevron-shaped left edge fills the right side and holds the chart. The footnote and source sit bottom-left, the page number bottom-right, and the 'Company Confidential' mark sits bottom-left in the file.

**Visuals**

Icons: (1) a navy outline shopping cart above the Engagement column, about x 7.4-11.8 percent, y 39.5-47.8 percent; (2) a navy outline cash register above the Purchase column, about x 28.6-33.3 percent, y 40-47.3 percent; (3) a navy outline cardboard box with a tape line above the Order Fulfillment column, about x 48.5-52.5 percent, y 39.7-48 percent. Each icon is a 1.5 pt navy line drawing with no fill. Stacked double-chevron-up symbols (grey-blue, about #7D92AD) sit to the right of each large percentage: about x 17.9-20.1 y 59-64.7 for ~75%, about x 39.1-41.4 for ~40%, and about x 58.7-61 for ~30%. Chart on the navy right panel: a column chart with title '2025 Average Customer Spend'; no printed axes or axis values; a thin white horizontal baseline from about x 72 to 98.3 at y 79.6. Two categories: Non-Digital Customers, a pale blue-grey bar (#CDE1E2) about x 76.2-80.9, y 76.7-79.6, labelled 1x; High & Medium Digitally Engaged, a teal bar (#66D7D1) about x 88.3-93.1, y 49-79.6, labelled 10x. Bar heights are relative: the 10x bar is about ten times the height of the 1x bar. A white dotted arrow with a solid triangular head rises diagonally from about x 82.3, y 67 to about x 85.9, y 55, above the 1x bar toward the 10x bar. No legend; no highlighted points beyond the teal bar.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with a light grey #E5E9EF panel on the left and a navy #1A4366 chevron panel on the right. Clean corporate look. Title is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #1A4366, about 5.6 percent of slide height, sentence case. Body text is regular Arial in navy #1A4366, about 2.9 percent of slide height. Column headings are bold Arial in teal-blue #2380A3 at about 3.7 percent. Large statistics are bold Arial in navy #1A4366 at about 6.7 percent. Chart labels are bold white (#FFFFFF) on navy, with 10x in teal #66D7D1 and 1x in pale #CDE1E2. Heading box has a 1 pt navy outline with square corners. Vertical column rules are 1 pt navy. Top tabs are rounded rectangles (about 1.5 percent corner radius) in #E5E9EF with labels in #CBD4DE; the active tab is navy #1A4366 with white bold label. Panels have square corners with no shadows or gradients. Footnote and source are grey #7E7E7E about 1.8 percent.

### Slide 38

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / Our digital experience is optimized for Pro workflows / Personalized for Key Roles / Executive / Purchaser / Installer / Project Manager / Pro Buying Journeys: / Stock & Flow Ordering / • Buy Again / • Product Recommendations / • BOPIS & Same-Day Delivery / Accessible from Anywhere, 24/7 / Complex Project Planning / • Specifications / • Configuration / • Bid Pricing / Company Confidential / 38

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge, with the active tab highlighted. A bold title sits at the top left below the strip. Below the title is a two-column grid: a narrow-gutter left column of two stacked grey panels (a short roles panel above a taller panel with the heading and two screenshots) and a tall right panel holding the buying journeys list. Text is left aligned with a left margin of about 4 percent. The footer confidentiality mark sits bottom left and the page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two photographs and a row of check-in-circle icons. The left photograph (x 7.1, y 55.2, w 22.5, h 33.3) shows a close-up of a person's hands holding a smartphone over blue denim jeans; the phone screen shows a blue banner reading 'Stock Up and Save More' with a product image, followed by a 'Top Categories' list. Lighting is soft and natural, dominant colours are denim blue, grey and white, and the image is a rectangular, uncropped box with square corners. The right photograph (x 32.3, y 55.2, w 22.4, h 33.3) shows a person's hands typing on a silver laptop keyboard seen from a three-quarter rear angle; the screen shows a website with a dark blue banner reading 'The Portable Speaker Perfected.', a row of white tiles and a 'Buy Again' heading. Lighting is soft daylight, dominant colours are silver, navy and beige, and the image is an uncropped rectangular box. Four outlined circle-with-tick icons in dark navy #1A4366 stroke sit to the left of Executive, Purchaser, Installer and Project Manager, at approximately x 26.1 y 23.5, x 40.0 y 23.5, x 26.1 y 29.6 and x 40.0 y 29.6, each about 1.9 by 3.3 percent. A tiny 220 by 220 pixel image sits at x 88.7, y 86.7, w 7.5, h 13.3 inside the right panel; it is not visible in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on a white slide with light grey-blue #E5E9EF panels. Title is bold dark navy #1A4366 in a bold sans-serif (Arial Bold), large, about 5.6 percent of slide height. Section headings are bold teal #2480A4 Arial Bold at about 4.1 percent. Body and list text is regular navy #1A4366 Arial at about 3.3 percent. Navigation tab labels are Arial at 1.9 percent; the active tab is navy #1A4366 with white bold text, and inactive tabs are #E5E9EF with pale grey-blue #CBD4DE text and faint outline icons. Panels are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows, and the photographs have no borders. Accents are teal #2480A4 headings and navy bullets.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Digital sales momentum is strong and / comes with structurally accretive margins / Omnichannel Experience / Category Leadership / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / ADI Digital Platform / Revenue FY 2018 - 2025 / >$1.4B / <$0.3B / +200bps / Higher gross margin rate / versus non-digital channels2 / 47% / Higher customer retention / versus non-digital customers3 / 2018 / 2019 / 2020 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 20241 / 20251 / Digital as % of / total revenue / 10% / 10% / 12% / 15% / 18% / 20% / 26% / 30% / 1 Results reflect Snap One acquisition beginning June 2024 / 2 Gross margin rate difference for digital platform sales versus non-digital sales for ADI Americas / 3 Customer retention measured based on ADI Americas customers Q1 2026 versus Q1 2025 / Source: Company Management Analysis / Company Confidential / 39

**Layout**

Five-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge with the second tab (Omnichannel Experience) highlighted in dark navy. A left-aligned two-line title sits below it at the top-left with a left-aligned blue subtitle beneath, a column bar chart occupies the lower-left, and a full-height dark navy panel fills the right 30 percent of the slide holding two large stat callouts; a grey band spans the lower-left under the chart carrying the digital percentage row, footnotes sit beneath it, and the page number 39 sits bottom right inside the dark panel.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical column chart, title 'ADI Digital Platform Revenue FY 2018 - 2025' (subtitle above chart). Eight columns, categories 2018, 2019, 2020, 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024 (footnote 1), 2025 (footnote 1); no y-axis, no gridlines, no numeric axis range printed. Single series, all columns #2380A3 with no data labels except callouts: '<$0.3B' above 2018 column and '>$1.4B' above 2025 column. Values for 2019 to 2024 are not printed; column heights are relative estimates (about 0.3B for 2018 rising to about 1.4B for 2025). Baseline: thin #1A4366 horizontal line under the columns. Below the chart, a row labelled 'Digital as % of total revenue' with eight outlined ellipses (stroke #2380A3, about 1 pt, no fill) containing printed values 10%, 10%, 12%, 15%, 18%, 20%, 26%, 30% in bold #1A4366, one per column in order 2018 to 2025. Legend: none. Highlighted points: none; the 2025 column and the >$1.4B callout are the tallest. Icons: five small grey line icons in the tabs (building/category icon, smartphone icon in highlighted tab, box icon, arrow icon, handshake icon, target icon) and no others.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate consulting style on white. Background white #FFFFFF. Title: bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #1A4366, two lines, title size about 5.6 percent of slide height. Subtitle and sub-caption: regular Arial in blue #2380A3, about 2.9 percent of slide height. Body and caption text in right panel: regular Arial in white #FFFFFF, about 2.9 percent. Large stat numerals: bold Arial in white #FFFFFF, about 8.4 percent; '>$1.4B' and '<$0.3B' bold navy #1A4366 about 5.2 percent. Footnotes and source: regular Arial in grey #7E7E7E, about 1.7 percent. Accent colour blue #2380A3 for columns and ellipse outlines. Right panel fill #2C4051 (dark slate). Lower band fill #E5E9EF. Navigation tabs: rounded pill shapes with corner radius about 1 percent of slide height, fill #E5E9EF, active tab #1A4366 with white bold label. No shadows, no gradients.

40

**Layout**

Four equal cards in a 2x2 grid below a top navigation strip of six tabs, with the slide title left-aligned near the top at about 3.9% from the left edge. Each card is about 43% wide and 33% high, split vertically: a coloured photo panel on the outer side (about 21% wide) and a white text panel with a thin coloured border on the inner side. Card text is left-aligned. A page number sits at bottom right and a small confidential line at bottom left.

**Visuals**

Four product photographs, one per card, all boxed inside their coloured panels and cropped by the white text boxes. (1) Top-left, E-Commerce & Mobile App: a laptop showing the ADI website (ADI logo top left, banner text 'Shop ADI and Earn Exciting Rewards', a white PlayStation-style controller and a camera product, a 'Best-Selling Categories' band below) with a smartphone in front showing a 'Shop Our IP Panoramic Cameras' category list; the laptop is cropped on the right by the white panel. (2) Top-right, Dynamic Pricing: a laptop on a dark navy panel showing a product page for a 'CI-T18F2S AcuSense 6MP IR Fixed Turret camera, 2.8mm Lens' with a dome camera photo, a '$225.00' price, Pickup and Delivery boxes and an Add to Cart button; the laptop base is at the bottom of the card and the screen is cropped by the white panel. (3) Bottom-left, Modern, Scalable ERP: a laptop on a slate-blue panel showing a dense grey and green ERP order-entry screen titled 'Order Entry: 15952570 (B&H Photo & Electronics Corp) Quote: N (New)' with tabs, forms and a data grid; the screen is cropped by the white panel. (4) Bottom-right, Search & Discovery: a laptop on a blue panel showing a product search results page with a product list and a grid of security cameras in the lower row; the screen is cropped by the white panel. Photographs are lit evenly with bright white screens and grey laptop bases; dominant colours are white, navy and teal. Each photo has no overlay or tint.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White slide background. Title in bold sans-serif (Arial Bold style) navy #1A4366, large. Body text in regular sans-serif (Arial style) navy #1A4366. Card headings are bold sans-serif navy #1A4366 and card body lines are regular navy #1A4366. Top navigation tabs are light grey #E5E9EF with grey-blue labels #CBD4DE, except the active tab which is navy #1A4366 with bold white label. Title size about 5.6% of slide height; card headings about 3.7%; card body about 3.3%; nav labels about 1.9%. Card borders are 1-2 px thin lines in each card's accent colour. Card panel colours: teal #66D7D1, navy #1A4366, slate #7C93AD, blue #2380A3. Shapes have square corners except the nav tabs which have rounded bottom corners. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

Six-tab section navigation runs across the top edge in a row of rounded pills, with the second tab ('Omnichannel Experience') active in navy. The title sits top-left at about 4% from the left edge, spanning two lines above the content. The main area is a left column of two navy KPI panels over a grey band of four outlined tiles, with the footnotes and source at bottom-left and the slide number bottom-right. A full-height photograph fills the right column from x 73.7%, with the content area left of it.

**Visuals**

Two navy KPI panels, each with a white outlined check-in-circle icon, a bold white two-line heading, a white horizontal rule, and a large white KPI figure with a label. Left panel: heading 'AI-Enabled / Product Search', figure '~25%', label 'Increase in / Digital Revenue' with superscript 1. Right panel: heading 'AI-Enabled / Pricing', figure '~30%', label 'Share of / Digital Revenue' with superscript 2. Bottom-left is a slate-blue block (#566E89) with the bold white text 'And we're accelerating AI capabilities...'. To its right, on a light grey-blue band (#E5E9EF), are four tiles with navy (#1A4366) 1 pt outlines and light grey fill, each with a teal circled plus icon (teal about #1E7FA6) at left, except the bottom-right tile which has no icon: 'Conversational Search' (top-left), 'Fast, Intelligent Quoting' (top-right), 'AI Answering' (bottom-left), '…and more underway' (bottom-right). Photograph at right: a laptop on a grey surface, screen showing an e-commerce site with a navy header bar, a banner reading 'Listen Everywhere Sonos Roam Portable Speaker' and a product image of a portable speaker, a 'Welcome Back, Rob!' greeting, tiles labelled Billing, My Quotes, My Lists and Notifications, and a 'Recommended for You' row of product thumbnails. Two hands are typing on the keyboard, seen from above and slightly right. Composition is a close, angled crop; it is full-bleed, filling the right column top to bottom with no border. Lighting is soft and natural with neutral greys and white, and the screen gives blue and navy accents. A placed 220 by 220 pixel image sits over the lower part of the photograph at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, not separately discernible in the render.

**Design**

Background is white (#FFFFFF). Title text is navy #1A4366, Arial Bold, sans-serif, about 5.6% of slide height, in sentence case, left aligned. Body and KPI text is white on navy panels (#FFFFFF), Arial regular for labels and Arial Bold for headings and figures; KPI figures are about 8.4% of slide height and headings about 3.7%. Tile text is navy #1A4366 Arial regular about 2.9% of slide height. Footnotes are grey #7E7E7E Arial about 1.7% of slide height. Accents: teal circle icons, slate #566E89 block, light grey #E5E9EF band and tiles. Navigation pills are light grey #E5E9EF with inactive labels in #CBD4DE, and the active pill is navy #1A4366 with white bold text. Shapes are flat with square corners on panels and tiles and rounded corners on the navigation pills; no shadows or gradients are visible.

### Slide 42

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Breaks a growth target into drivers (spend per customer, price realization, digital adoption) with a clear target callout, making the logic easy to follow.
- Review: Weaknesses are moderate: slide 42 has an awkward white band across the middle, uneven empty space and an unequal driver column, several diagram slides run small text, and some content repeats (the pillar slides at 6, 22 and 65, the target table at 19 and 60, the cost savings on 18, 53 and 54).
- Suggested repair: Tidy slide 42 by removing the stray white band and balancing the driver columns, and enlarge or simplify the tiny logo walls on slide 26, the small text on slide 47 and the footnote type throughout.

42

**Layout**

Six-tab section navigation runs across the top edge with the title below it, flush left at about 3.9% from the left edge. The body is a two-zone layout: three driver columns (Digital Adoption, Total Spend per Customer, Price Realization), each with a vertical navy rule and a numbered-free header, sit on a light grey band on the left, and a full-height navy chevron panel on the right holds two key metrics. A footnote block sits bottom-left at about 3.2% from the left edge, and the page number is bottom-right at about 97.7% from the left edge.

**Visuals**

Three vertical navy lines, each with a filled navy dot at its top and bottom ends, run from about 34.5% to 78.2% of slide height at x about 9.0%, 29.7% and 51.3%. Two outlined teal-blue plus-in-circle icons sit on the second and third lines at about y 41.7% centre, one at x about 29.7% and one at x about 51.3%, each about 3% of width in diameter, stroke about 2 px in teal-blue #1F86A8. Six small grey line icons sit inside the top tabs, from left to right: a bar-and-people chart, a phone, a cube with arrows, an upward arrow in a circle, a hand, and a target. The active tab icon is a white phone. A photograph-type image box (220 by 220 pixels) is placed at x 88.7%, y 86.7%, 7.5% by 13.3%; it is not visibly rendered on the slide. No chart, table or photograph is shown.

**Design**

Background: White #FFFFFF, with a light grey band #E5E9EF behind the lower-left body area and a navy #1A4366 chevron panel on the right. Clean corporate style on white with a light grey band and a navy panel. The title is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height per line, with about 1.25 line spacing. Body text is regular Arial in navy #1A4366, about 2.9% of slide height, with about 1.2 line spacing. Column headers are Arial Bold, about 3.7% of slide height, navy #1A4366. Key metric figures are Arial Bold about 5.9% of slide height in mint-teal #66D7D1. Metric captions are Arial regular about 2.5% in white. The navigation tabs are rounded rectangles with corner radius about 1.5% of slide height, fill #E5E9EF, with the active tab filled navy #1A4366 and white bold text; inactive tab labels are regular Arial in #CBD4DE. Footnotes are Arial about 1.7% of slide height in grey #7E7E7E. Page number is Arial about 1.9% in #2C4051. No shadows or gradients. Vertical rules are navy #1A4366, about 2 px wide.

### Slide 43

**Text**

ADI’s premier omnichannel platform / drives profitable growth / 1 / Industry-leading omnichannel platform / 2 / AI-enabled digital experience poised / to scale / 3 / Enabling profitable growth with each / digital transaction / Company Confidential / 43

**Layout**

Two-column split: a photograph fills the left 30% of the slide and a full-height navy panel fills the right 70%. The title sits top-left of the panel at about x 35.6%, y 6%, left-aligned in two lines. Three stacked rows of numbered cards start at x 34.8% and run down the panel, each row about 21% high with equal gaps of about 3%. Slide number sits bottom-right at about x 97.7%, y 96.5%. A small confidentiality line sits bottom-left at about x 1.6%, y 95.8%.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the left 30% of the slide, full-height and unboxed, cropped at the panel edge. It shows a laptop, screen angled toward the viewer, on a light grey surface. The screen displays an ADI e-commerce product page for the LUMA LUM-520-IP-DFTW 520 Series Vandal 5MP Compact Dome IP Outdoor Camera, 2.8mm Fixed Lens, White. The page shows a white dome camera product image centred on the screen, a header with the ADI logo and navigation items SHOP, BRANDS, DEALS, SOLUTIONS & SERVICES, a price of $223.39 with MSRP $447.99, feature bullets, a pickup and shipping panel and a blue Add to Cart button. A pair of hands rests on the keyboard at the lower right of the photo. Lighting is soft and natural with a cool neutral palette of white, grey and dark charcoal, with blue accents from the screen. A small 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at about x 88.7%, y 86.7%, w 7.5%, h 13.3%, overlapping the photo's lower right area. Navy #1A4366 tint overlay at 10% opacity covers the photo. Three numbered rows: each row is a white rectangle with a teal #2380A3 square at its left holding a white bold number 1, 2 or 3. No connectors or arrows.

**Design**

Background is solid navy #1A4366 across the whole slide. Titles are white #FFFFFF bold Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif), title cap height about 5.9% of slide height, set in two lines. Numbers are white bold Arial about 6.7% of slide height. Row text is teal #2380A3 bold Arial about 4.4% of slide height. Accent colour is teal #2380A3. Row cards are plain white rectangles with square corners, no border, no shadow, no gradient. Teal number squares are flat #2380A3 with no border. Footer text is grey #7F7F7F italic Arial about 1.9% of slide height. Slide number is light #FFFFFF Arial about 1.9% of slide height. Margins are roughly 3.5% on the right of the panel.

### Slide 44

**Text**

Trusted Partner to Deliver
Be the trusted Partner
counted on to deliver
when it matters
Allie Copeland

44

**Layout**

Single-column title slide on a full-bleed patterned background. All text is left-aligned to a common left margin at about 6.9% of slide width, with the eyebrow line, three-line headline and presenter name block stacked in the middle-left and lower-left. The ADI logo sits in the bottom-right corner at about 82% to 94% width and 83% to 92% height, and the page number sits in the bottom-right corner below it at about 97.7% x and 96.5% y.

**Visuals**

Full-slide background illustration: a repeating geometric pattern of thin outlined chevron and triangle motifs drawn in slightly darker blue lines over a blue gradient, running diagonally from upper-left to lower-right. The pattern is full-bleed, not boxed, with no visible crop and no photograph subject. Lighting is flat and even; the gradient is darker navy at lower-left and brighter teal-blue at upper-right. Dominant colours are navy blue and teal-blue. A white ADI logo (wordmark with slanted slash strokes) sits bottom-right on top of the pattern. A second 220 by 220 pixel image box sits at the same lower-right position, overlapping the logo; it has no visible content against the background.

**Design**

Background: Blue gradient from navy #163F62 at lower-left to teal-blue #2384A6 at upper-right, with a faint geometric line pattern overlay. Full-bleed blue gradient background with a faint tone-on-tone geometric line pattern overlay. Headline and presenter name are white (#FFFFFF). The eyebrow line 'Trusted Partner to Deliver' and its accent are teal #66D7D1. Typefaces are Arial (Helvetica-style grotesque sans-serif): bold for the headline and name, regular for the eyebrow and role line. Headline text is about 7.5% of slide height per line, presenter name about 4.4%, eyebrow about 4.1%, role line about 3.3%. Line and shape styles: none apart from the pattern lines. No corner radii, shadows or visible gradients on shapes other than the background gradient.

### Slide 45

**Text**

Big projects are tough and require tailored support
Project pipeline review with ADI sales rep
ADI consults on bill of materials - gets supplier project pricing
Place order via Electronic Data Interchange (EDI)
Register next big project with ADI
Send quote, win job
Receive product on schedule based on project needs
Trusted by enterprise customers when it matters for the big projects
Company Confidential
45

**Layout**

Single white slide with the title top-left at about 4% from the left edge and 6% from the top, left-aligned. Below it, six equal portrait photos sit in one horizontal row, each about 11.3% wide and 26.6% tall, spaced evenly across the middle band from about 10% to 88% of the width. Each photo has a thin teal connector with a dot at each end, with its label placed above the row (photos 1, 3 and 5) or below it (photos 2, 4 and 6), alternating. A dashed teal arrow runs under the row at about 85% height, with a bold-led tagline beneath it. The footer 'Company Confidential' sits bottom-left and the page number '45' bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Six photographs in a single row, each a vertical 330 by 440 pixel rectangle with no border and no overlay. 1) A man with short brown hair and stubble, wearing a rolled-up denim shirt, holds a phone to his ear and looks down at a laptop on a wooden table; warm indoor background, soft daylight from the left. 2) A laptop screen, angled, showing a light web 'Checkout' page with an 'Order Summary' panel and a blue button, on a wooden desk with a potted plant at right. 3) A smiling woman with dark hair, round glasses and a black headset microphone, wearing a sage-green top, in a bright office; she is cropped at the waist. 4) Two people shaking hands in close-up, one in a dark charcoal shirt and one in a light blue sleeve, cropped at the torso, muted office background. 5) A man with a beard in a white shirt, looking down and typing on a silver laptop on a dark wooden table, with a phone at lower left. 6) A man in a navy polo shirt holding a taped cardboard box against his side, cropped at the head, with a blurred warehouse background. Photos are cool-to-warm neutral tones with navy, teal-grey and tan dominant colours. Connectors: six straight vertical teal lines (#2380A3, about 1.5 pt) each ending in a filled teal dot about 1% of the slide height across; each line runs from its label dot down or up into the photo edge. Dashed arrow: a horizontal dashed teal line (#2380A3) from about 10.6% to 88% of the width with a filled triangular arrowhead at the right end. One further 220 by 220 pixel image is recorded at about x 88.7%, y 86.7% but is not visible in the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF) with no gradient, shadow or texture. Title and labels in bold Arial-style grotesque sans-serif in dark navy-blue #1A4366; the title is about 5.6% of slide height and the bold labels about 2.9%. Tagline 'Trusted' in bold and the rest in regular weight, about 3.7% of slide height, same navy #1A4366. Footer 'Company Confidential' in italic Arial, about 1.9%, grey #7F7F7F. Page number in regular Arial about 1.9%, slate #2C4051. Accent colour teal blue #2380A3 for connectors, dots and dashed arrow. Shapes have square corners; photos are unbordered rectangles.

46

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge in grey pills, with the first tab active in navy. The title sits left-aligned below it at roughly 4% left margin and about 6% from the top. A three-piece jigsaw graphic occupies the left-centre: a tall navy block on the left and three stacked colour blocks on the right, each joined by interlocking knobs. Three bold statements are left-aligned in a column to the right of the puzzle, each beside its matching block. A small ADI logo sits bottom right with the page number below it at the far right corner; 'Company Confidential' sits bottom left.

**Visuals**

Diagram/illustration: a puzzle-style graphic of four rectangles. Left: a tall navy (#1A4366) rectangle containing the white ADI logo (wordmark with a slanted stroke, approx. 13.7% of slide width) above the text 'Indispensable Partner of Choice For 40+ Years'. Right column of three stacked rectangles, top to bottom: teal #31BCB5 labelled 'Integrator Partners', mid-blue #2380A3 labelled 'Supplier Partners', slate grey #566E89 labelled 'Technology Partners'. Each right block has a semicircular knob where it meets its neighbour: a navy knob on the left edge of the teal block and the slate block, a blue knob on the left edge of the blue block and on the top edge of the blue block (into teal) and bottom edge (into slate); knobs are filled circles with no stroke and no shadow, and they fit into notches of the adjacent shape. No chart, table or photograph. Navigation tab icons (six small line icons, one per tab, in pale grey on grey tabs, white on the active tab): people figures group for Category Leadership, a mobile phone outline for Omnichannel Experience, a box with circular arrows for Exclusive Brands, an arrow in a circle for Expansion Categories, a handshake for Value-added Services, and a target with concentric rings for Execution Excellence. Logo: ADI navy wordmark bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient or pattern. Title and tab labels in navy #1A4366 (active tab label white). Subtitles in the three puzzle colours: teal #31BCB5, blue #2380A3, slate #566E89. The puzzle labels and the left panel text are white. Typeface is Arial (Arial Bold for titles, labels and statements; Arial Regular for the left panel text), a sans-serif. Title is about 5.6% of slide height and bold; statements and labels are about 3.3% to 4.1% of slide height in bold; tab labels about 1.9%. Shapes have sharp square corners for the puzzle blocks, rounded pill-like corners on the navigation tabs, no shadows and no gradients. Page number and confidentiality line are small, grey or dark slate.

47

**Layout**

Three full-height vertical panels, left to right: a light grey-blue left panel (about 0 to 33.7% of width), a mid blue-grey centre panel (about 33.2 to 66.9%) and a navy right panel (about 66.2 to 100%), all running from about 20% of slide height to the bottom edge. A row of six rounded navigation tabs sits across the top (about 0 to 4% height), with the first tab in navy and the rest in pale grey. The two-line bold title sits top-left at about 3.9% left margin, between about 6% and 18% of height, left aligned. Footnotes are bottom-left in small grey text and the page number is bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Diagram. Left panel: a 'Customer Segments' header over three outlined rectangles labelled Type A, Type B and Type C (stroke navy #1A4366, no fill, columns about 5.3% wide, running from about 38% to 91% of height). A 'Channel' column label sits left of the grid with four white row bands labelled Security & Fire, ResiAV, ProAV and DataComm, separated by pale grey #E5E9EF gaps. A filled slate #566E89 circle marker sits in the Security & Fire row inside the Type B column. A slate #566E89 arrow leaves the circle, running right across the centre panel boundary to the centre box. Centre panel: a white box with a slate #566E89 header reading 'One team from customer order to post-install', containing five stacked pale grey #E5E9EF bars labelled Stores, Sales, Customer Support, Operations and Supply Chain from top to bottom. A white block arrow leaves the centre box right-ward into the navy right panel. Right panel: two large white statistics, '54 NPS' with caption 'Excellent customer experience' (superscript 1) and '+MSD%' with caption 'Spend per customer growth (5-yr CAGR)' (superscript 2). Tab icons are small line icons: people (Category Leadership, white), phone or tablet, box with arrows, circular arrow, handshake, and target; all in pale grey #CBD4DE on the grey tabs. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with flat panels of #E5E9EF (left), #B0BECE (centre) and #1A4366 (right) behind the content. Clean corporate consulting style. White base with three flat colour panels: pale grey #E5E9EF, slate blue-grey #B0BECE and navy #1A4366. Title text navy #1A4366, bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 5.6% of slide height per line. Panel headings in regular Arial, about 3.3% of height: navy #1A4366 on the left, dark slate #2C4051 in the centre, white on the navy panel. Table labels and captions in regular Arial about 2.5% of height; row labels #2C4051. Accent colour slate #566E89 for headers, markers and arrows, and navy #1A4366 for bold labels. Shapes are flat with thin navy outlines on the segment columns and no shadows, gradients or rounded corners on the main content. Navigation tabs have rounded corners and no shadow.

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge with the active tab Omnichannel Experience highlighted in navy. The two-line bold navy title sits flush left at about 3.9% from the left edge, starting near 6% from the top. Four checkmark bullets stack in a single left column at about 9.7% from the left. A North America map fills the right half, with a legend label at top right. A full-width navy band across the bottom holds four centred statistics in equal columns, with a footnote at lower left and the page number 48 at lower right.

**Visuals**

Illustration: light grey (#F2F2F2) outline map of North America, including Alaska, Canada, the contiguous United States, Mexico, Central America, the Caribbean and Hawaii, drawn as filled shapes with thin lighter outlines, no labels. Over the contiguous US and southern Canada sit numerous small navy (#1A4366) filled circles with white outlines marking store locations, densest in the eastern US and the Great Lakes region, with isolated dots in the west, Texas, Florida, Hawaii and the Caribbean. Legend: one navy dot followed by the text 160 North America Stores. Icons: four identical navy circled checkmark icons (outline circle with check stroke, stroke about 0.3% of slide height) beside the first four bullets. Six small grey line icons sit inside the navigation tabs; the active tab icon is white. Icons in the stat band: none visible. ### Slide 49

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / We deliver a premier / store experience for Pros / One-stop shop across categories / Products & experience forward / Catered to local market demand / Access to training & expertise / Company Confidential / 49

**Layout**

Split layout: a white text column occupies the left half and a full-height store photograph fills the right half edge to edge. A row of six rounded navigation tabs runs across the top of the slide, with the active tab Omnichannel Experience in navy. The title sits in the upper-middle of the left column, left-aligned at about 6.9% from the left edge, with four check-marked points stacked beneath it at about 12% from the left. The footer Company Confidential sits bottom-left and the page number 49 sits bottom-right, both near the bottom margin.

**Visuals**

Photograph occupying the right half, full-bleed to the top, bottom and right edges and cut off by the slide's left boundary at about 51% of the width. Subject: the interior of a big-box retail store, with long aisles of metal shelving stocked with boxed products, white and blue packages, cardboard cartons and pegboard display walls. Overhead, rows of white linear ceiling lights hang beneath exposed grey ductwork and trusses, with white square support pillars and dark navy aisle signage hanging from the ceiling. The camera is at a high-angle, elevated view looking down a receding aisle, and the composition is deep, with the vanishing point near the centre. Lighting is bright, even and fluorescent, with no visible shadows. Dominant colours are warm beige, light grey, white and muted blue, with the floor a tan-brown. There is no overlay or tint. Four navy check-in-circle icons sit in the left column, one beside each point, drawn as outlined circles with checkmarks. Six small monochrome line icons sit inside the top navigation tabs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF, with the store photograph covering the right half of the slide. Clean, minimal corporate look on a white background. Titles and body text are navy #1A4366, with the check icons in the same navy outline. Inactive navigation tabs are pale grey #E5E9EF with light grey-blue labels #CBD4DE, and the active tab is solid navy #1A4366 with bold white text. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif), with the title in bold and the body in regular weight. The title is about 5.6% of slide height and the body text about 3.3%, with navigation labels about 1.9%. Shapes are rounded-rectangle pills with corner radii of roughly one-third of their height. No shadows or gradients are visible.

**Layout**

Six section tabs run across the top edge in a single row of rounded pills, with the current section 'Expansion Categories' highlighted in dark navy. The title sits left-aligned below the tabs, spanning most of the width. Below it, five steps rise from lower left to upper right in a staircase of five stacked blocks, each block offset right and up from the previous one, with a short description beneath each block. A full-width dark footer band at the bottom carries a centred bold takeaway line and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Staircase diagram of five rectangular blocks with squared-off corners, each stepping up and right: Trust (dark teal #1A607A, lower left, bottom edge at about y 70.5%), Convenience (turquoise #31BCB5), New Products (grey-blue #7C93AD), Services (blue #2380A3), and Expansion (dark navy #1A4366, top right, drawn as a pentagon with a pointed right end like an arrow tip). Each block carries a white bold label and a navy description below it, with no connecting arrows. Above the staircase the nav bar has six pill-shaped tabs: five light grey #E5E9EF with grey text and one dark navy #1A4366 with white text. Each tab has a small grey line icon at its left: a buildings/city silhouette (Category Leadership), a phone or tablet (Omnichannel Experience), a box with circular arrows (Exclusive Brands), a white circled upward arrow (Expansion Categories), a handshake (Value-added Services), and a target with concentric rings (Execution Excellence). A small image (220 by 220 pixels) sits at bottom right inside the footer band at x 88.7, y 86.7, with no clearly readable content in the render.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient. Title and nav text use Arial Bold in navy #1A4366, title about 5.6% of slide height. Step labels are Arial Bold white at about 3.3% of slide height. Descriptions are Arial Regular in navy #1A4366 at about 2.9%. Footer takeaway is Arial Bold white at about 3.7%. Nav tab labels are Arial at about 1.9%, in #CBD4DE on inactive tabs. Blocks have small corner radii on the bottom-right corners only and no shadows. Accent colours step from dark teal to turquoise to grey-blue to blue to navy.

### Slide 51

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / An expanding portfolio of services is designed to reinforce / ADI’s enduring value proposition / Project / Overview / Multimillion-dollar camera project / with enterprise customer for large / state corrections department / ADI / Services / Provided / Bill of materials development / System design / Project kitting and staging / Project-based quoting / Project-based credit line / 20+ services offered, focused on solving pain points for Pros / Company Confidential / 51

**Layout**

A six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge, with the title in two lines below it, left-aligned at about 3.9% from the left edge. Below the title, the slide splits into two columns: a light grey left panel holding a label-and-text stack (a 'Project Overview' row above an 'ADI Services Provided' checklist, each label right-aligned against a vertical rule) and a photograph filling the right half. A full-width navy band runs along the bottom with a centred one-line statement and a page number at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Photograph in the right half (x 50 to 100, y 20.1 to 86.7): a man with a short beard and glasses pushed up on his head, wearing a dark zip-neck jacket, looks down and works with both hands on a white camera-mount device; in the foreground left a white robotic camera arm and clear acrylic post are visible; behind him are several blurred monitors showing warm orange, yellow and white camera imagery, with cables and dark equipment. Lighting is warm and practical from the screens with soft falloff. Dominant colours are dark charcoal, orange-yellow and white with navy-grey shadows. The image is full-bleed to its panel with no border, overlay or tint. Five check-in-circle icons, outline style in navy #1A4366, sit left of the five bullet lines (about x 17.8 to 19.7, each about 3.4% high). Six navigation tab icons, grey #CBD4DE on inactive tabs and white on the active tab: a city skyline or chess-piece-like building, a phone or tablet, a boxed item with arrows, an upward arrow in a circle, a handshake, and a target.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF across the top, with light grey #E5E9EF panel on the left and navy #2C4051 footer band along the bottom. White background; light grey #E5E9EF left panel; inactive nav tabs #E5E9EF with grey text #CBD4DE; active tab navy #1A4366 with white bold text; navy footer band #2C4051. Title text and body text are navy #1A4366; the footer statement is white bold; the confidentiality line is grey #7F7F7F. Typeface is Arial (Arial Bold for titles, labels and the footer statement; Arial Regular for body and bullets; Arial Italic for the confidentiality mark), a sans-serif. Title is about 5.6% of slide height per line, body text about 2.9%, labels about 3.7%. Vertical divider rules are navy #1A4366, about 0.3% wide. Tabs are rounded rectangles with small corner radius and no shadow. No gradients.

**Layout**

Six-tab navigation strip runs across the top edge, with the title in two left-aligned lines below it at a left margin of about 4 percent. A full-width navy band sits in the middle of the slide and holds a centred heading, two milestone labels with a chevron arrow between them, and a monitor and laptop photo. Five equal columns with vertical rule dividers occupy the lower third, each with a blue heading and dark body text, and the ADI logo sits bottom right above the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs and a row of shapes. (1) Left photo: a beige CRT monitor with a grey stand, shown front-on, its screen displaying a green-on-black text terminal titled MASTER MENU with numbered green menu lines and a red status bar along the bottom; the photo is boxed, not cropped, on a white background. (2) Right photo: a silver open laptop in three-quarter view showing an ERP order-entry screen (header Order Entry: 15552570 (B&H Photo & Electronics Corp) Quote: N (New)) with toolbars, form fields, a green-header line-item grid and a blue-grey interface; boxed on white with a soft grey base shadow. (3) Three right-pointing chevrons in a row between the milestone labels, pale blue-grey, evenly spaced, forming a progression from REZI Spin to ADIG Spin. (4) Four thin vertical blue divider lines separating the five lower columns. (5) ADI logo: bold navy wordmark with a blue accent, bottom right. (6) Navigation tabs: five pale grey rounded tabs and one blue active tab, each with a faint grey icon at its left. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with a navy #1A4366 band across the middle of the slide. White background with a single navy band (#1A4366) across the middle third. Title navy #1A4366 in Arial Bold, large (about 5.6 percent of slide height per line). Subheading and milestone labels white Arial Bold, about 5.2 percent for the heading and 3.7 percent for milestone labels; years in white Arial Italic. Lower column headings blue #2380A3 Arial Bold about 3.7 percent; body text navy #1A4366 Arial regular about 2.8 percent. Navigation tab labels #CBD4DE Arial about 1.9 percent, active tab label white bold. Accent colour #2380A3. Tabs are rounded pill-like rectangles with no shadow; dividers are thin 1 to 2 pixel blue lines. Photos have no rounded corners or shadows except the faint laptop base shadow.

**Layout**

Full-width slide with a row of six section tabs across the top edge (y 0 to 4%), the title left-aligned below them at about y 6%, and a three-circle equation row centred in the middle band (y 30% to 80%) with a footer of two footnote lines at bottom left and the ADI logo and slide number at bottom right. Left margin about 4%, right margin about 2%; the title starts at x 3.9%.

**Visuals**

Three large circles in a horizontal row joined by two small operator circles, forming the equation 'In-year Focus' + 'Near-term Focus' = '$80M+ run-rate operating savings targeted to be achieved by end of 2027'. Left circle and middle circle are light grey-blue #CBD4DE filled, each with a dark navy bold heading and three centred dark navy lines. The plus operator circle is navy #1A4366 with a white plus sign. The equals operator circle is teal #2380A3 with a white equals sign. The right circle is solid navy #1A4366 with a white bold $80M+ and white italic explanatory text. No chart, table or photograph. Top navigation: five light grey #E5E9EF rounded tabs with faint grey icons and labels, and one teal #2380A3 tab for Execution Excellence with a white target icon and bold white label, marking the current section. A small ADI wordmark logo in navy sits bottom right. No other graphics.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold navy #1A4366 sans-serif (Arial Bold style), about 5.6% of slide height. Circle headings in bold navy #1A4366 Arial, about 4.4% of slide height. Circle body lines in regular navy #1A4366 Arial, about 3.3% of slide height. Right circle text white, with the $80M+ figure in bold Arial about 5.2% and explanatory lines in italic Arial about 3.3%. Operator symbols white Arial about 7.5%. Footnotes in grey #7E7E7E Arial about 1.7%. Page number in #2C4051 Arial about 1.9%. Tab labels in #CBD4DE Arial about 1.9% on light tabs, white bold on the teal tab. Circles are flat with no outline or shadow, no gradients. Tabs have rounded bottom corners and no outline or shadow.

### Slide 54

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck states one four-part case on slide 6 and carries it consistently through the section dividers, the top navigation tabs (slides 25 to 54) and the close on slide 65, so the audience can follow the argument across 69 pages.

**Text**

Category Leadership / Omnichannel Experience / Exclusive Brands / Expansion Categories / Value-added Services / Execution Excellence / Significant cost reductions with most already actioned to / deliver in-year impact / Actioned / complete / In-action / still on-going / $30M+ / In-year planned operating expense reduction 1 / Org structure alignment / Streamlined org with 2% reduction in force completed in Q2 2026 / Continued SNPO synergy capture / Executed SNPO synergy capture in areas dependent on recent system upgrades / Store footprint redundancies / Consolidating redundant store footprint with ~10 store combinations in 2026 (5 already completed) / 1 Gross operating savings estimate, does not include cost to achieve / Note: Forward-looking target. Results may vary, see slide 2 for more information. / ADI / 54 / Company Confidential

**Layout**

Six-tab section navigator runs across the top edge, with the title in two lines at the upper left below it. A tall navy panel occupies the left column (about 2% to 26.5% width) and three stacked horizontal bands fill the right side (about 45% to 98% width), each with a teal label chevron and a light text area. A legend sits at upper right under the navigator, footnotes sit bottom left, the ADI logo sits bottom right and the slide number sits at the bottom right corner, left-aligned to the content margin at about 3.8%.

**Visuals**

Icons: the active top tab 'Execution Excellence' shows a target icon in white (concentric circles with an arrow) at about x 85 y 0.5, size about 3% by 3.5%. Inactive tabs show faint grey line icons at their left ends: a bar/people-style icon (Category Leadership), a phone-style icon (Omnichannel Experience), a cube with arrows (Exclusive Brands), an upward arrow in a circle (Expansion Categories), and a handshake (Value-added Services). Legend icons: a teal (#2380A3) outlined circle containing a check mark at about x 80.8 y 15.3, meaning Actioned / complete; a teal circular two-arrow refresh icon at about x 80.8 y 19.5, meaning In-action / still on-going. Row icons: teal outlined check-in-circle icons at about x 50.3 y 24.9 (row 1), x 50.3 y 47.0 (row 2); a teal refresh icon at about x 50.3 y 73.6 (row 3). Logo: ADI wordmark in navy (#1A4366), letterforms in bold condensed sans, bottom right at x 91.7 y 91.2, 5.0% by 3.6%. A 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at x 88.7 y 86.7 (7.5% by 13.3%) with no visible content in the rendered slide. No photographs or charts.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title is bold Arial-style sans-serif in navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height, left aligned, two lines with tight leading. Body text is regular Arial-style sans-serif in navy #1A4366 at about 3.7% of height for the statements, and bold white for the chevron labels. Accent colour is teal #2380A3 for chevrons and the active tab, icons and legend. Light panels are pale blue-grey #CDE1E2 with no border. Navigator tabs are rounded rectangles in light grey #E5E9EF with faint grey text #CBD4DE at about 1.9% of height; the active tab is teal #2380A3 with white bold text. Shapes are flat with no shadows or gradients; chevron label shapes have a pointed right edge. Footnotes are grey #7E7E7E regular sans at about 1.7% of height; page number is #2C4051 at about 1.9%.

**Layout**

Five-tab section navigator runs across the top edge, with the title in two lines at upper left below it and a two-item legend at upper right. Below the title, a full-height navy panel occupies the left column (about 2% to 27% of width) and five stacked horizontal rows occupy the right column (about 28% to 98% of width), each row pairing a teal chevron label with a light-teal bar. Footnotes sit bottom left, the ADI logo bottom right and the slide number at the far bottom right; left margin about 2% to 4%.

**Visuals**

Five-row process/action list, no chart or table. Left navy panel holds a large white figure $80M+ with caption Near-term planned run-rate operating expense reduction with superscript 1. Each row has a teal (#2380A3) chevron-tipped label box on the left and a pale teal (#CDE1E2) bar on the right. Icons: a teal outline check-in-circle icon sits beside row 1 (In-year 2026 actions); teal outline circular two-arrow refresh icons sit beside rows 2 to 5 (about 50% of width, about 3% wide and 5% tall each, centred vertically in each row). Legend at upper right: a teal check-in-circle icon (about 81% x, 14% y, about 2% wide) labelled Complete by end of 2026, and a teal circular refresh-arrows icon (about 81% x, 19% y) labelled On-going actions in 2027. Top navigator tabs carry faded grey-blue icons (about 3% wide) for the five inactive tabs: a people/shelf icon for Category Leadership, a phone icon for Omnichannel Experience, a box icon for Exclusive Brands, an arrow-in-circle icon for Expansion Categories and a handshake icon for Value-added Services; the active Execution Excellence tab shows a white target icon. Bottom right: ADI logo in navy, wordmark about 5% wide. The file also contains a 220 by 220 pixel image box at about 89% x, 87% y (7.5% by 13.3%) with no visible rendering on the slide. No photographs. Footnote text is the only other content.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate slide on white with navy (#1A4366) headline and body text, teal (#2380A3) label boxes and icons, and pale teal (#CDE1E2) row bars. Inactive navigator tabs are light grey (#E5E9EF) with faded grey-blue labels (#CBD4DE); the active tab is teal (#2380A3) with white bold text. Typeface is Arial (Helvetica-like grotesque sans-serif), bold for title, row labels and the $80M+ figure, regular for body and footnotes. Title text is about 5.6% of slide height, bold navy; the row body text and captions are about 3.3% to 3.7%; footnotes about 1.7%; navigator text about 1.9%. Row labels are bold white; row body text regular navy. Shapes are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadows, gradients or outlines; label boxes end in a right-pointing chevron point.

**Layout**

A full-height photograph fills the left 30 percent of the slide and a navy panel fills the right 70 percent. The title sits top-left of the navy panel at about 6 percent from the top, left-aligned at x 35.6. Three equal white cards are stacked vertically at x 34.9 to 94.9, each about 21.3 percent tall with gaps of about 2.4 percent, each with a teal number block at its left edge. The footer 'Company Confidential' sits bottom-left over the photograph and the page number sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Photograph at left, x 0 to 30 percent, full height and full-bleed to the left and top and bottom edges, 879 by 1649 pixels. Subject: an interior view of a consumer electronics retail store with grey pegboard walls and shelving. Upper area shows headphones and cables hung on hooks in blister packs. Middle and lower shelves hold stacked tan cardboard boxes, boxed audio products with white, red and black packaging (one blue box reads 'Russound'), and a dark boxed item. Floor is a beige-grey tile. Composition is a diagonal view from upper left toward lower right with shelves receding; the photo is cropped at the panel edge. Lighting is even overhead store light. Dominant colours: tan brown, white, red, black, grey. A navy #1A4366 tint at 25 percent opacity covers the photograph. Three numbered cards are flat rectangles with no visible shadow: white #FFFFFF boxes with teal #2380A3 squares at left containing white numerals 1, 2 and 3. A small 220 by 220 pixel image sits at the bottom right (x 88.7, y 86.7, 7.5 by 13.3 percent) and is not visibly discernible on the rendered slide.

**Design**

Background: Solid navy #1A4366 with a full-height photograph occupying the left 30 percent. Navy #1A4366 background with a 25 percent navy tint over the photograph strip. Titles are white #FFFFFF bold Arial sans-serif, about 5.6 percent of slide height for the title and 6.7 percent for the numerals. Card text is teal #2380A3 bold Arial sans-serif at about 4.4 percent of slide height. Footer is grey #7F7F7F italic Arial at about 1.9 percent. Page number is white Arial at about 1.9 percent. Cards and number blocks are square-cornered with no outline, shadow or gradient.

Presenter name is Arial Bold white at about 4.4%; role line is Arial regular white at about 3.3%. Company Confidential note is Arial Italic grey #7F7F7F at about 1.9%. $4.8B

22.3%

$295M

Company Confidential
58

**Layout**

Four full-width horizontal rows stacked evenly in the middle of the slide, each row split into a pale left label panel (about 64% of the width) and a dark navy right value panel with a chevron-shaped left edge. The title sits top-left at about 6% from the top and wraps to two lines, with a one-line subtitle beneath it. The note and source lines are left-aligned at the bottom-left, the ADI logo sits bottom-right, and the page number sits bottom-right at the far corner. Left margin is about 3.9% and right margin about 2.3%.

**Visuals**

Four row graphics, each a pale grey-blue rectangle (left, label panel) joined to a navy chevron-ended rectangle (right, value panel). Row 1: label circle with the numeral 1 and the label Revenue, value $4.8B. Row 2: numeral 2, label Gross Profit Margin, value 22.3%. Row 3: numeral 3, label Standalone Adj. EBITDA, value $295M. Row 4: numeral 4, label Standalone Adj. EBITDA Margin, value 6.2%. Each numeral is set inside a thin navy outlined circle with a light weight numeral. No charts, photographs or illustrations. The ADI logo is a navy wordmark, bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with no gradient. Title and subtitle are navy #1A4366 in Arial Bold (title) and Arial Regular (subtitle), sans-serif. Body labels are navy #1A4366 in Arial Bold. Values are white #FFFFFF in Arial Bold. Title is about 5.6% of slide height, subtitle about 3.7%, row labels about 5.2%, values about 7.5%, footnotes about 1.7%. Row label panels are pale #E5E9EF; value panels are navy #1A4366 with a chevron notch on the left edge. Shapes have square corners, no shadows, no outlines. Numeral circles have thin navy outlines. Rows are separated by white gaps of about 1.5% of slide height.

**Layout**

Two-by-two grid of equal grey chart panels occupying the middle of the slide, with a left-aligned bold title at the top, a left-aligned subtitle beneath it, and footer source notes at bottom left. The ADI logo sits bottom right and the page number sits at the far bottom right; left margin about 4% and right margin about 4%. Gutters are about 1.2% horizontally and 2.1% vertically between panels.

**Visuals**

Four vertical bar charts, each with a centred bold panel title, a grey '$M, USD' unit label at upper left, flat navy bars with the value printed in white bold centred inside each bar, a thin navy baseline, and year labels 2023, 2024, 2025 beneath. No y-axis, gridlines or scale are drawn, so bar heights are not labelled and no range is printed. No legend. No highlighted points. Revenue: 2023 $3,570, 2024 $4,197, 2025 $4,784 (bars ascending). Adjusted EBITDA: 2023 $238, 2024 $286, 2025 $318. Capital Expenditures: 2023 $27, 2024 $25, 2025 $54. Adjusted EBITDA less CapEx: 2023 $211, 2024 $261, 2025 $264. Bar heights are roughly proportional to values within each panel. Bars are navy #1A4366 on light grey-blue #E5E9EF panels. The ADI logo is a small navy wordmark, bottom right, not overlapping any other element. A second, invisible 220 by 220 pixel image sits behind it with no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with four light grey-blue panels (#E5E9EF) arranged in a 2x2 grid. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in regular Arial-style sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 2.9%. Chart titles in bold Arial-style sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 2.9%. Unit labels in regular sans-serif, grey #7F7F7F, about 2.0%. Bar value labels in bold sans-serif, white #FFFFFF, about 2.3%. Year labels in regular sans-serif, navy #1A4366, about 2.0%. Footnotes in regular sans-serif, grey #7F7F7F, about 1.7%. Page number in regular sans-serif, #2C4051, about 1.9%. Panels and bars are flat with square corners, no shadows, no gradients, no borders.

**Layout**

Full-width four-row table on a white 16:9 slide: a title in two lines at the top left, a column-header row above the grid, and a teal chevron-shaped summary panel on the right. Left margin about 3.9% and right edge about 96%; the row labels sit in a light band at left, then the 2025A column, a narrow unboxed arrow column, the 2030 Targets column, and the teal panel. Footnotes sit bottom left below the grid, the ADI logo bottom right, and the page number '60' at the bottom far right.

**Visuals**

Grid-style table with 4 numbered rows plus a summary panel; no chart. Columns: (a) row labels with a numbered outline circle 1, 2, 3, 4; (b) '2025A' column, slate blue #7C93AD boxes with white bold values: row 1 $4.8B, row 2 22.3%, row 3 $295M, row 4 6.2%; (c) middle unboxed column with a teal #2380A3 thin horizontal arrow per row (about 8% slide width, arrowhead at right) and a label above and below the arrow: row 1 '4 - 6%' over 'CAGR'; row 2 '40 - 50' over 'BPS'; row 3 '>10%' over 'CAGR'; row 4 '~200' over 'BPS'; (d) '2030 Targets' column, navy #1A4366 boxes with white bold values: row 1 ~$6B, row 2 >22.7%, row 3 ~$500M, row 4 >8%. Rows are light grey #E5E9EF bands separated by thin white horizontal gaps. Right of the table, a teal #2380A3 chevron panel with a notched left edge pointing right and a flat right edge; inside it an outlined white circle containing the numeral 5 (thin white stroke, about 0.3% width), then the label 'Target Cash Flow From Operations' with superscript 2, large white bold '$1B+', and 'Cumulative cash flow from FY2026-2030' in white regular. Each row's numbered circle is a thin navy #1A4366 outline with a thin navy numeral about 4.4% wide. Small ADI logo (navy wordmark, #1A4366) in the bottom right corner, box about x91.7 y91.2 w5 h3.6, over a transparent 220 by 220 px image frame at x88.7 y86.7 w7.5 h13.3. No photographs, no icons other than the numbered circles and ADI logo.
