61

**Layout**

Six-column floating-bar step chart on a white field: a title at top-left in two lines, a subtitle beneath it, and a horizontal navy baseline at about 77.8% of height. Columns run left to right as 2025 (6.2%), Mix, Pricing, Business Transformation, Operational Excellence and 2030 Target (>8%), with each step's box rising higher than the last. Grouping brackets sit under the baseline: Gross Margin Expansion spans Mix and Pricing, Operating Leverage spans Business Transformation and Operational Excellence. Footnotes are bottom-left, the ADI logo is bottom-right above the page number, and the margins are about 4% on left and right.

**Visuals**

Chart: floating step (waterfall-style) column diagram titled Standalone Adj. EBITDA Margin. No axes, scale or gridlines are printed; the column heights are schematic, not to scale. Series of six columns from left to right: 2025 light grey-blue column labelled 6.2% AEBITDA Margin; Mix white box with slate header labelled Mix and three bullets (Exclusive Brands growth, Digital sales growth, Expansion category growth); Pricing white box with header labelled Pricing and two bullets (AI-enabled pricing, Exclusive Brands pricing management); Business Transformation white box with header and four bullets (Org alignment, Real estate footprint, Platform consolidation, AI / automation); Operational Excellence white box with header and two bullets (Operating expense leverage, Continuous improvement); 2030 Target dark navy column labelled >8% AEBITDA Margin. Two grouping bars below the baseline: Gross Margin Expansion (spanning Mix and Pricing) and Operating Leverage (spanning Business Transformation and Operational Excellence). Highlighted points: the 2030 Target column is the only dark, fully saturated column. Legend: none. Logo: ADI wordmark in navy, bottom-right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

Pro Forma
Capitalization1

$400M

$1,000M

$850M

62

**Layout**

Two-line bold title sits top-left at x 3.9% and y 6.1%, left-aligned on a white slide with about 4% side margins. Below it a full-width light grey panel (x 3.2%, y 21.7%, w 93.7%, h 66.7%) holds a centred subtitle and two bordered columns: a financial table at left (x 4.9% to 50%) and a Key Credit Highlights box at right (x 51.9% to 95.2%), with a white Pro Forma value column inside the table. Footnotes sit bottom-left below the panel, the ADI logo is bottom-right and the page number is at the bottom-right corner.

Two-panel graphic with no chart. Left: a table with navy outline (about 3 pt stroke) on light grey #E5E9EF, with an unlabelled label column and a white value column headed Pro Forma Capitalization with superscript 1. Rows: Cash & Cash Equivalents $150M; Unsecured Debt $400M; Secured Debt $600M; a thin navy rule; Total Debt $1,000M; a second thin navy rule; Total Net Debt $850M (bold). Right: a box with an outline in navy and a solid navy header bar #1A4366 reading Key Credit Highlights in white, below which three bullets are listed in navy text on grey. Logo: the navy ADI wordmark at bottom-right. A 220 by 220 px image box at x 88.7% y 86.7% is not visible on the slide and appears empty.

### Slide 63

**Text**

ADI will adopt a strategic and disciplined approach to
capital allocation
1
Near-term
deleveraging
• Obtain and maintain investment grade rating
• Build financial flexibility to use balance sheet over time
2
Invest in
organic growth
• Accelerate strategic growth priorities
• Make investments with high ROIC
3
Targeted M&A
• Near-term focus on accretive tuck-ins
• Acquisition strategy will evolve over medium term as
deleveraging progresses
4
Return of capital
• Evaluate returning capital to shareholders
Company Confidential
63

**Layout**

Four full-width horizontal rows stacked vertically between the title and the footer, each row split into a dark navy chevron block on the left (numbered circle plus bold label) and a pale grey panel on the right holding bullet points. The title is left-aligned at the top-left margin in two lines; the footer 'Company Confidential' sits bottom-left and the page number '63' sits bottom-right, both small.

**Visuals**

Four numbered step rows, each consisting of a navy chevron block with a thin white outlined circle containing numerals 1, 2, 3 and 4 (white thin sans-serif numerals), followed by bold white labels: 'Near-term deleveraging' (row 1), 'Invest in organic growth' (row 2), 'Targeted M&A' (row 3), 'Return of capital' (row 4). Right of each chevron is a light grey-blue panel carrying navy bullet points. Row 1 bullets: 'Obtain and maintain investment grade rating'; 'Build financial flexibility to use balance sheet over time'. Row 2 bullets: 'Accelerate strategic growth priorities'; 'Make investments with high ROIC'. Row 3 bullets: 'Near-term focus on accretive tuck-ins'; 'Acquisition strategy will evolve over medium term as deleveraging progresses'. Row 4 bullet: 'Evaluate returning capital to shareholders'. A placed image (220 by 220 pixels) sits at the bottom-right corner area but shows no visible content in the render. No charts, photographs or logos are visible.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on a plain white background. Titles and body text are navy #1A4366; step labels are white bold; the chevron blocks are navy #1A4366 and the bullet panels are pale grey-blue #E5E9EF. Typeface is Arial (Arial Bold for title and labels, Arial Regular for bullets), a sans-serif. Title text is about 5.6% of slide height; body and label text about 3.7%; footer and page number about 1.9%. Shapes are sharp-cornered rectangles with a pointed chevron tip on the right edge of each navy block; no outlines, shadows or gradients. Rows are separated by thin white gaps.

### Slide 65

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck states one four-part case on slide 6 and carries it consistently through the section dividers, the top navigation tabs (slides 25 to 54) and the close on slide 65, so the audience can follow the argument across 69 pages.

**Text**

ADI is a differentiated leader in an attractive market / driving margin expansion and cash flow generation / 1 / Category- / leading / Positions / 2 / Preeminent / Omnichannel / One-stop Shop / 3 / Multiple Levers / for Above / Market Growth / 4 / Attractive / Margin Upside / Potential & Cash / Flow / Company Confidential / 65

**Layout**

Four equal rounded cards sit in one horizontal row across the middle-upper slide, evenly spaced with about 2.3% gaps and roughly 5.3% side margins, below a two-line bold title that starts at the left margin near the top (x 3.9%). The ADI logo sits bottom right above the page number, and 'Company Confidential' sits bottom left, with the slide number at the bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

Four solid navy rounded rectangle cards (#1A4366) with large corner radii, each containing a thin teal (#66D7D1) circle outline holding a numeral 1, 2, 3 and 4 in light teal sans-serif, with white bold centred labels below. Bottom right: the ADI logo wordmark in navy #1A4366 (145 by 60 pixel image, boxed, no tint). A 220 by 220 pixel image box sits behind the logo area at x 88.7, y 86.7 and shows no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or pattern. Title is bold sans-serif (Arial Bold) in navy #1A4366 at about 5.6% of slide height, left aligned, two lines with tight leading. Card labels are bold Arial in white #FFFFFF at about 3.7% of slide height, centred, with about 4.4% line spacing. Accent colour is teal #66D7D1 for the circle outlines and numerals. Cards are flat navy with large rounded corners (radius about 16% of card width) and no shadow. Footer text is Arial in grey #7F7F7F (confidential) and slate #2C4051 (page number), both about 1.9% of slide height.

### Slide 66

**Text**

For virtual attendee Q&A, please send questions to:
investorrelations@adiglobal.com
Company Confidential
66

**Layout**

Centred composition on a plain white field. The ADI logo sits in the upper-middle, with the two-line Q&A instruction centred beneath it, and the confidentiality line bottom-left and the page number bottom-right. There is no title and no header band. The footer and page number sit near the bottom edge with small margins.

**Visuals**

One logo: the ADI wordmark in solid navy #1D4266, spanning roughly x 23.7% to 72.8% and y 24.7% to 61.1% of the slide. The letters are flat geometric shapes: a slanted parallelogram stroke with a separate triangle forming the A, a D built from a vertical bar with a curved outer bowl and an inner white counter, and a vertical bar forming the I. Thin white gaps separate the letters. A second 220 by 220 pixel image is placed at the bottom-right (x 88.7%, y 86.7%, 7.5% by 13.3%) but shows nothing visible in the render, so it appears blank or white.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient, texture or photograph. The logo and the main instruction text are navy (#1A4366 for the text, #1D4266 for the logo). The email address is bold and the question line is regular, both in Arial-style sans-serif. Text is set large for a slide, about 4.4% of the slide height for the instruction lines, with the footer at about 1.9%. No shapes, lines, shadows, corner radii or accent colours.

**Text**

Non-GAAP EBITDA Reconciliation
Reconciliation from Reported EBITDA per financial reports to Standalone Adjusted EBITDA
($M) / 2023 / 2024 / 2025
GAAP Income from operations / $238 / $195 / $212
Stock-based compensation expense / 7 / 13 / 18
Acquisition and integration costs / - / 12 / 8
Restructuring expenses / 12 / 19 / 8
Other Adjustments / - / 11 / -
Reported Adjusted Income from Operations / $257 / $250 / $246
Depreciation & Amortization / 18 / 68 / 113
Reported Segment Adjusted EBITDA / $275 / $318 / $359
Allocation of Corporate costs / (47) / (76) / (49)
Snap One transaction and integration costs / - / 32 / -
Allocated stock-based compensation expense / 8 / 10 / 7
Other Adjustments / 2 / 2 / 1
Adjusted EBITDA (per Form 10) / $238 / $286 / $318
Incremental Recurring Expenses 1 / / / (23)
Standalone Adjusted EBITDA / / / $295
1 Estimated Incremental Recurring Expenses represent costs above Allocation of Corporate Costs. As a separate public company,ADI expects to incur incremental costs within certain corporate functions / including Finance, IT, Legal, Human Resources and other general and administrative related functions.
Company Confidential
68
ADI

**Layout**

Single-column slide with the title top-left at about 3.9% from the left and 6.1% from the top, the subtitle directly beneath it, and a centred financial table spanning about 8.2% to 90.1% of the width. The footnote sits bottom-left at about 4% from the left, with the ADI logo and page number 68 bottom-right; there is no footer bar.

**Visuals**

Table with 4 columns and 17 rows. Column 1 is the row label column, wide, about 8.2% to 45.9% of width. Columns 2 to 4 are year columns for 2023, 2024 and 2025, each about 14.7% wide, with centred numbers. Header row: ($M) in italic, then 2023, 2024, 2025 in bold white on teal. Rows, values per year: GAAP Income from operations (bold) $238, $195, $212; Stock-based compensation expense 7, 13, 18; Acquisition and integration costs -, 12, 8; Restructuring expenses 12, 19, 8; Other Adjustments -, 11, -; Reported Adjusted Income from Operations (bold, teal band) $257, $250, $246; Depreciation & Amortization 18, 68, 113; Reported Segment Adjusted EBITDA (bold, teal band) $275, $318, $359; Allocation of Corporate costs (47), (76), (49); Snap One transaction and integration costs -, 32, -; Allocated stock-based compensation expense 8, 10, 7; Other Adjustments 2, 2, 1; Adjusted EBITDA (per Form 10) (bold, teal band) $238, $286, $318; Incremental Recurring Expenses with superscript 1 (bold, pale cyan row; 2023 and 2024 cells grey #D0D0D0 with no value; 2025 cell pale cyan with (23)); Standalone Adjusted EBITDA (bold, teal band; 2023 and 2024 cells dark grey #808080 with no value; 2025 cell teal with $295). Thin light grey horizontal rules separate the plain rows and white vertical gaps separate the columns. No chart, photograph or illustration. Small ADI logo bottom-right in dark blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif, dark blue #1A4366, about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle in regular Arial, #1A4366, about 3.3% of slide height. Table body text regular Arial, #194164, about 2.3% of slide height; bold rows for totals. Accent teal #2380A3 for header and total bands; pale cyan #E0F7F6 and light grey #D0D0D0 for the estimate row; dark grey #808080 for empty cells. Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners and no shadows or gradients. Footnote in grey #7F7F7F, Arial, about 1.7% of slide height.

69

**Layout**

Single-slide table layout on a white field: the title sits top-left at about 6% from the top with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned to a margin of about 4% from the left edge. A full-width financial table spans from about 8% to 90% horizontally, starting at about 23% from the top and ending near 84%, with a footnote block at the bottom left, the ADI logo at bottom right and the page number at the far bottom right corner.

**Visuals**

One financial reconciliation table with 4 columns: row label column (about 8% to 45% of width) and three numeric columns headed 2023, 2024 and 2025 (centred at about 52%, 67% and 82% of width). Header row is ($M) in italic followed by 2023, 2024, 2025 in bold white on teal blue. Rows in order: Net (loss) income 62 / (18) / (261) in bold; Income Taxes 50 / 25 / 11; Depreciation & Amortization 22 / 71 / 115; Interest expense, net 14 / 24 / 42; Indemnification agreement expense 67 / 79 / 364; Stock-based compensation expense 15 / 23 / 24; Restructuring, impairment, and extinguishment 13 / 22 / 9; Transaction related expenses - / 45 / 16; Purchase accounting fair value adjustment - / 9 / -; Other Adjustments (5) / 6 / (2); Adjusted EBITDA (per Form 10) $238 / $286 / $318 in white on teal band; Incremental Recurring Expenses superscript 1 with blank 2023 and 2024 cells in grey and (23) in 2025 on pale cyan; Standalone Adjusted EBITDA with dark grey blanks for 2023 and 2024 and $295 for 2025 on teal band; Allocation of Corporate Costs 47 / 76 / 49; Snap One transaction and integration costs - / (32) / -; Allocated stock-based compensation expense (8) / (10) / (7); Other Adjustments (2) / (2) / (1); Incremental recurring expenses blank grey for 2023 and 2024 and 23 for 2025; Reported Segment Adjusted EBITDA $275 / $318 / $359 in white on teal band. No chart, no photograph. The ADI logo is a dark blue wordmark at bottom right.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, no gradients or shadows. Title and subtitle in dark blue #1A4366. Body table text in dark blue #1A4366 (some labels #194164). Accent teal blue #2380A3 for header and total bands with white bold text. Pale cyan #E0F7F6 highlight for Incremental Recurring Expenses row, light grey #D0D0D0 for empty cells, dark grey #808080 for two blank cells in Standalone Adjusted EBITDA row. Typeface is Arial (Arial-BoldMT for bold, ArialMT regular, Arial-ItalicMT italic), sans-serif. Title is bold and about 5.6% of slide height; subtitle regular about 3.3%; table text about 2.2%; footnote about 1.7%. Table rows are separated by thin light grey horizontal rules; header and total bands are solid fills with thin white vertical separators between columns. No rounded corners.
